# SimLab coverage checklist, part B: Drone Lab and figures

This is the second half of SimLab's master coverage checklist: one row for every behaviour, value, message, defect
fix and figure rule that the Drone Lab research and the Colorfigures research found. Every later phase checks its
work against these rows. An item counts as covered only when its verify method has passed.

Part A (`coverage-checklist-a.md`) covers the brief itself (SPEC), Learn Arduino, the Robotics Bench and the audit of
the current build. This part covers:

- **DLA**: everything in `drone-lab-a.md` (the Drone Lab engine, physics, controls, range, views, sound, URL hooks
  and phone layout, the four original crafts with their options, parts, CLI, ranges and quizzes, the 31 defects,
  the accuracy notes and the Appendix A golden values).
- **DLB**: everything in `drone-lab-b.md` for Figs 05-13, with `drone-lab-b-calc-matrix.md` as the golden data.
- **CF**: the brief's "every figure must be" list, `colorfigures-guide.md` and the Colorfigures skill (look,
  rotation, see-through, labels, exploded view, pressable parts, truth, self-contained build, phone, the embed
  bridge, localisation, dark mode, verification, the 25 pitfalls, the engine rules and the Appendix E defects).

Global brief items (host-page i18n, accessibility, the dashboard, deploy, the README of Colorfigures prompts) live
in part A. They are repeated here only where they bind a figure or the Drone Lab.

## How to read a row

- **id**: prefix plus a three-digit number, numbered in table order within each prefix.
- **statement**: one testable sentence saying what SimLab must or must not do. Text in quotes (labels, messages,
  CLI lines) and the numbers given (constants, timings, colours, sizes) are kept exactly, unless
  `docs/DECISIONS.md` records a correction.
  - "must not copy: …" marks a defect in an original site or in the current baseline. The rest of the sentence
    gives the corrected behaviour.
  - "must not …" without "copy" marks a known failure pattern (mostly the Colorfigures pitfalls) and its fix.
  - Where a lesson, snippet or tile was wrong, the row states the correct fact. Firmware values follow the
    research's accuracy notes; the Betaflight, ArduPilot, INAV and MAVSDK points were checked again against their
    documentation while this list was written.
- **source**: the file, then the section (`§`, `Fig`, `Appendix`), then the lines. When rows were merged, every
  source is listed, separated by semicolons. A section or line list without a file name continues the file named
  before it.
- **priority**:
  - **must**: the brief, a feature of an original site, or a defect fix.
  - **should**: a nice-to-have, or a design choice the research recommends without a brief need behind it.
  - CF bridge and architecture rows are must where they fix an Appendix E defect or carry a brief requirement
    (localisation, dark mode, keyboard, phone, self-contained pages), and should otherwise.
- **verify**:
  - **unit**: a test on data or logic (calculator values, validators, config).
  - **e2e**: a browser test drives the page and checks the result.
  - **screenshot**: a shot is taken and inspected or compared.
  - **review**: a person reads the code, text or snippet against the row.

## Rules that apply to every table

- **Golden values.** For each craft, the calculator must reproduce `drone-lab-a.md` Appendix A and
  `drone-lab-b-calc-matrix.md` exactly at their display precision, for every battery × motor × prop combination
  (the verdict compared on its first 140 characters, as the matrix records it). A value may differ only where a row
  here corrects it and `docs/DECISIONS.md` records why.
- **Fixed, not copied.** The originals' defects are fixed, never reproduced for fidelity. Where a row keeps an
  original value that later research shows to be wrong, the correction wins and goes into `docs/DECISIONS.md`.
- **Own code.** Facts, formulas, constants, timings and teaching messages are reused. The originals' source files,
  watermarks and credits are not. The Colorfigures MIT licence and credit are kept.
- **Gaps.** Needs the brief states but no source settles are listed at the end, with the conflicts between sources.
  They need a decision in `docs/DECISIONS.md` before the phase that builds them.

| DLA-059 | The lesson defaults must be: hint "design it, build it, then fly it", noun "drone", palette tomato, log name betaflight. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L189-192 | must | unit |
| DLA-060 | The bench geometry defaults must be: mat 280 mm, benchH 120 mm, benchZ 5 mm, benchUnit = mat/280, explode = 16·benchUnit mm per layer. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L193-197 | must | unit |
| DLA-061 | The flight defaults must be: flyScale 3, flyVB 5200 mm, camZ 0.8, ceiling 12 m, drag 0.045, hitR 0.14 m, FPV FOV 105°, camTilt 25°, sagK 1. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L198-209 | must | unit |
| DLA-062 | A craft must open fully built with every part placed the right way round, the smoke, motor and accelerometer checks passed, the battery plugged, the default mode selected and no flight, so a visitor can press Fly at once. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L176-182 | must | e2e |
| DLA-063 | Part records must carry id, name, short, z (layer), after (prerequisites), polar, opts [right, wrong] and what, and must keep the ids FRAME, MOTORS, ESC, WIRES, FC, XT60, CAM, RX, VTX, TOP, BATT, PROPS where the craft has those parts. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L217 | must | unit |
| DLA-064 | Motor positions must be listed as [x, y, label, CW or CCW]; a craft with none gets no motor test. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L218 | must | unit |
| DLA-065 | The part prerequisites for the tests must default to WIRES+FC (motor check), XT60+ESC (smoke stopper) and BATT+XT60 (plugging in), overridable per craft. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L220 | must | unit |
| DLA-066 | The fault smoke point should default to [mat/2−46, mat/2+13, 18], the stopper lead end to [−0.3·mat, 0, 8] and the motor-test arrow height to 48 mm, overridable per craft. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L222-224 | should | screenshot |
| DLA-067 | Part tags must be data ([id, [x, y, z], dx, dy]) so each label pill is anchored to its part. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L227 | must | unit |
| DLA-068 | Each craft's modes must be data ({ key: { title, code, hint, alt?, acro? } }), defaulting to alt, angle, acro and race, with "range" added whenever the craft defines range goals. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L178, L229 | must | unit |
| DLA-069 | A craft must be able to replace the multirotor model with its own physics that can write stick commands and call the multirotor model (used by the whoop, long range and Figs 05-13). | drone-lab-a.md §2 L231 | must | unit |
| DLA-070 | A craft must be able to add its own iso drawing in flight and its own flight stats. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L232-233 | must | e2e |
| DLA-071 | Race gates must be data ([x, y, headingDeg]) defaulting to 5 gates of 1.6 × 1.6 m, overridable per craft. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L234-235 | must | unit |
| DLA-072 | A part must count as fitted only when placed, not leaving and its drop animation has finished; a part counts as in place (for prerequisites) when placed and not leaving. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L243-244 | must | unit |
| DLA-073 | The bench animation should step by real elapsed time with dt capped at 50 ms and should go idle when nothing is moving. | drone-lab-a.md §2 L245-246 | should | unit |
| DLA-074 | The generic calculator must take n = the craft's motor count, else the number of motor positions, else 4. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L256 | must | unit |
| DLA-075 | The calculator must use nominal pack voltage V = cells × 3.7. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L258 | must | unit |
| DLA-076 | The calculator must compute no-load krpm = KV × V / 1000. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L259 | must | unit |
| DLA-077 | Full-throttle thrust per motor in grams must be kThrust × prop pf × krpm², with kThrust 1.06 by default. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L257, L260 | must | unit |
| DLA-078 | All-up weight must be the sum of the fixed parts' grams + n × motor grams + n × prop grams + battery grams. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L261 | must | unit |
| DLA-079 | Thrust to weight must be n × thrust / AUW. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L262 | must | unit |
| DLA-080 | Hover power must be AUW / (gPerW × prop eff) with gPerW 5.4 by default, and hover current that power / V. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L263-264 | must | unit |
| DLA-081 | Hover time in minutes must be (mAh/1000 × 0.8) / hoverA × 60 (80 % usable capacity). | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L265 | must | unit |
| DLA-082 | Aggressive flight time must be hover time × 0.42 for the generic calculator. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L266 | must | unit |
| DLA-083 | Max current must be n × thrust / (2.0 × prop eff) / V. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L267 | must | unit |
| DLA-084 | The over-spin flag must be KV × cells > maxKvCells, default 1750 × 6 × 1.15 = 12075. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L268 | must | unit |
| DLA-085 | The weak flag must be T:W < minTW, default 5. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L269 | must | unit |
| DLA-086 | Electrical cost must be the fixed Electrical prices + n × motor price + battery price. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L270 | must | unit |
| DLA-087 | Mechanical cost must be the fixed Mechanical prices + prop price × ceil(n/2), because a prop price is per pair. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L271 | must | unit |
| DLA-088 | Hover throttle must be sqrt(AUW / (n × thrust)), that is sqrt(1/T:W), because thrust scales with throttle squared. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L272 | must | unit |
| DLA-089 | Catalogue data that no code reads (the motors' forCells) should either drive a real check or be dropped; SimLab should not carry dead catalogue fields. | drone-lab-a.md §3.1 L275 | should | review |
| DLA-090 | The cinewhoop calculator must use thrust = 0.24 × prop pf × motor sz × krpm² and hover power AUW / (3.4 × prop eff), with n = 4. | drone-lab-a.md §3.2 L277-281 | must | unit |
| DLA-091 | The cinewhoop calculator must use aggressive time = hover time × 0.85 and max current = n × thrust / (1.7 × prop eff) / V. | drone-lab-a.md §3.2 L279-280 | must | unit |
| DLA-092 | The cinewhoop calculator must flag over when cells > the motor's maxCells, flag weak when T:W < 3, and cost props at price × 2. | drone-lab-a.md §3.2 L280-281 | must | unit |
| DLA-093 | The whoop calculator must use kThrust 0.00304, gPerW 3.6, over when KV × cells > 28000, and minTW 2.6. | drone-lab-a.md §3.3 L285-290 | must | unit |
| DLA-094 | The long-range calculator must use kThrust 1.6, gPerW 8, over when KV × cells > 1300 × 6 × 1.2 = 9360, and minTW 3.5. | drone-lab-a.md §3.3 L285-290 | must | unit |
| DLA-095 | The six default tiles must read "All-up weight" {round(auw)} g, "Thrust : weight" {tw.toFixed(1)} : 1, "Hover throttle" {round(hoverThr×100)}%, "Max current" {round(maxA)} A, "Hover time" {hoverMin.toFixed(1)} min, and the aggressive-time tile {flyMin.toFixed(1)} min. | drone-lab-a.md §3.4 L294-297; SPEC.md L114 | must | e2e |
| DLA-096 | The default over warning must read "{kv} KV on {cells}S spins the props far past what the motors and ESC can take: expect hot motors and a desync.". | drone-lab-a.md §3.4 L299-300 | must | unit |
| DLA-097 | The default weak warning must read "Thrust to weight {tw.toFixed(1)}:1 is too low for this kind of flying.". | drone-lab-a.md §3.4 L301 | must | unit |
| DLA-098 | With no warning the panel must show green "Parts match. Plenty of punch." when T:W > 10, else "Parts match. Good all-round build.". | drone-lab-a.md §3.4 L301-302 | must | unit |
| DLA-099 | The cost table must have the columns Category, Parts, Cost, an Electrical row (count = fixed electrical rows + 2), a Mechanical row (count = fixed mechanical rows + 1), and "Total (approx.)", with whole-dollar amounts such as "$287". | drone-lab-a.md §3.4 L304-306 | must | e2e |
| DLA-100 | Changing a design select while flying armed must be refused with "Land and disarm before changing parts" and the select must revert to its previous value. | drone-lab-a.md §3.4 L308 | must | e2e |
| DLA-101 | Changing a design select on the bench must update every tile, warning, cost row, bench stat and the figure at once and play the `click` effect. | drone-lab-a.md §3.4 L308-309 | must | e2e |
| DLA-102 | Golden data: the quad5 calculator must reproduce all 27 rows of drone-lab-a.md Appendix A quad5 (AUW, g/motor, T:W, hover throttle, max A, hover min, fly min, flags, costs, warning text) at the shown precision, except rows changed by a correction recorded in docs/DECISIONS.md. | drone-lab-a.md Appendix A L1297-1327 | must | unit |
| DLA-103 | Golden data: the whoop calculator must reproduce all 27 rows of drone-lab-a.md Appendix A whoop exactly, except rows changed by a correction recorded in docs/DECISIONS.md. | drone-lab-a.md Appendix A L1329-1359 | must | unit |
| DLA-104 | Golden data: the cinewhoop calculator must reproduce all 27 rows of drone-lab-a.md Appendix A cinewhoop exactly, except rows changed by a correction recorded in docs/DECISIONS.md. | drone-lab-a.md Appendix A L1361-1391 | must | unit |
| DLA-105 | Golden data: the long-range calculator must reproduce all 27 rows of drone-lab-a.md Appendix A longrange exactly, except rows changed by a correction recorded in docs/DECISIONS.md. | drone-lab-a.md Appendix A L1393-1423 | must | unit |
| DLA-106 | The default designs must be quad5 6s1100 / 2306-1750 / 5x4.3x3, whoop 1s300 / 0702-25000 / 31mm-4, cinewhoop 4s1100 / 2004-3000 / 3x3x3, longrange 6s1p4000 / 2807-1300 / 7x3.5x2. | drone-lab-a.md Appendix A L1297, L1329, L1361, L1393 | must | unit |
| DLA-107 | Every golden row whose expected value SimLab changes on purpose (new label, new check, corrected constant) must be listed with its old and new value in the golden test table and in docs/DECISIONS.md. | drone-lab-a.md Appendix A L1293-1295; SPEC.md L153 | must | review |

## DLA · Bench build, plug-in and safety tests

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DLA-108 | A polar part must offer two orientation choices, the right one and the mistake, and orientation 1 must always be the mistake. | drone-lab-a.md §4.1 L319-320 | must | unit |
| DLA-109 | "Add next part ▾" must list every part, not only the next one: one chip per non-polar part ("{short}") and two per polar part ("{short}: {opt}"). | drone-lab-a.md §4.1 L321-322 | must | e2e |
| DLA-110 | A part chip must show as current when that part is placed with that orientation, and as unavailable with the reason "{short} goes on after {missing short, lowercased}" when its prerequisite is missing. | drone-lab-a.md §4.1 L322-323 | must | e2e |
| DLA-111 | Each part's layer value must set how far it lifts in the exploded view. | drone-lab-a.md §4.1 L324 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-112 | Adding a part in flight must be refused with "Land and go back to the bench first". | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L328 | must | e2e |
| DLA-113 | Adding or re-orienting any part other than the battery while the battery is plugged must be refused with "Unplug the battery first: never work on a live quad". | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L329 | must | e2e |
| DLA-114 | Adding a part with the exploded view open must be refused with "Close the exploded view first". | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L330 | must | e2e |
| DLA-115 | Adding a part that is already on in that orientation (or a non-polar part already on) must answer "{short} is already on" with the tick effect. | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L331 | must | e2e |
| DLA-116 | Choosing the other orientation of a placed polar part must re-orient it: the part re-drops with the whoosh effect, re-orienting the XT60 clears the smoke check and re-orienting the FC clears the accelerometer calibration. | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L331-333 | must | e2e |
| DLA-117 | Adding a part whose prerequisite is missing must be refused with "{short} goes on after {missing short, lowercased}". | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L336 | must | e2e |
| DLA-118 | Adding the props before the smoke stopper test (unless the craft has none) and the motor direction check (when it has motors) have passed must be refused with "Props go on LAST: run the smoke stopper test and the motor direction check first" for 3.2 s. | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L337-338 | must | e2e |
| DLA-119 | Placing a part must select it and play whoosh; placing the XT60 clears the smoke check, placing the motor wires clears the motor check (a fresh wiring job always has one reversed motor), placing the FC clears the calibration. | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L339-340 | must | e2e |
| DLA-120 | The add-part rules must be checked in this order: in flight, live battery, exploded view, already on or re-orient, missing prerequisite, props last, then place. | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L326-340 | must | unit |
| DLA-121 | A placed part must drop from 70·u mm above (u = mat/280) with ease-in-out over 0.45 s and play the place effect when it lands. | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L342-343 | must | unit |
| DLA-122 | The exploded view must lift each part by ease(ex)·layer·explode, with ex moving at 1.5 per second. | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L343-344 | must | unit |
| DLA-123 | A part being removed should fade out at 2.6 per second. | drone-lab-a.md §4.2 L344 | should | unit |
| DLA-124 | "Take it apart" in flight must answer "Land first". | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L348 | must | e2e |
| DLA-125 | "Take it apart" must unplug silently, close the exploded view, clear the queue, selection and fault, remove parts top layer first staggered by 0.05 s, clear all three checks, and play whoosh. | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L348-350 | must | e2e |
| DLA-126 | "Take it apart" with nothing placed must answer "Already a box of parts" with tick. | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L349 | must | e2e |
| DLA-127 | "Build it for me" in flight must answer "Land first"; on the bench it must unplug silently, close the exploded view, and queue every missing part in list order with the props last, one part every 0.35 s. | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L351-352 | must | e2e |
| DLA-128 | "Build it for me" on a complete craft must answer "Already built" with tick. | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L352 | must | e2e |
| DLA-129 | "Exploded view" in flight must answer "Land first"; on the bench it must unplug silently and toggle the exploded view. | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L354 | must | e2e |
| DLA-130 | "Plug / unplug battery" in flight must answer "Use Back to the bench first". | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L355 | must | e2e |
| DLA-131 | Unplugging must play the unplug effect and log "battery unplugged". | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L355-356 | must | e2e |
| DLA-132 | Plugging in without the battery and its power lead fitted must answer "Mount the battery and its power lead first". | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L356 | must | e2e |
| DLA-133 | Plugging in must play the plug effect and log "battery plugged: {V} V" with V = cells × 4.2 printed with at most two decimals (25.2 V on 6S). | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L357; §10 defect 22 | must | unit |
| DLA-134 | Plugging in with the XT60 cap reversed and no passed smoke test must raise the cap fault: pop, then sizzle 120 ms later, and the toast "POP: the capacitor was in backwards and vented. A smoke stopper would have caught it. Unplug, replace it" for 4.2 s. | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L357-359 | must | e2e |
| DLA-135 | The cap fault must turn the capacitor brown (#7a6a5a) and raise smoke puffs at the craft's smoke point (about 8 per second, rising 30 mm/s, radius 3+9·age, opacity 0.6−0.55·age, life 1.1 s). | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L360-361 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-136 | A healthy plug-in must play the power effect and say "Beep beep beep: ESCs armed-ready, FC and VTX on". | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L361 | must | e2e |
| DLA-137 | While powered (plugged, FC fitted, no fault) the FC LED must glow blue and the VTX, air unit and GPS LEDs must glow. | drone-lab-a.md §4.3 L362 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-138 | The smoke stopper test without the power lead and ESC fitted must answer "Solder the power lead and the ESC first", and in flight "Land first". | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L367 | must | e2e |
| DLA-139 | Running the smoke stopper test must unplug silently, play click, log "$ smoke stopper between battery and quad..." and run for 2.2 s. | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L368 | must | e2e |
| DLA-140 | The smoke stopper must be drawn as a dark box (30 × 18 × 12 mm × u) at (0.09·mat, 0.71·mat) with a clear lamp labelled "STOP" and a red lead running to the craft. | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L369-370 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-141 | The stopper lamp must blink orange (on while sin(12t) > 0, glow radius 7) when there is no short and glow solid red (radius 16) when the cap is reversed. | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L370-371 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-142 | With the cap reversed the smoke stopper test must log "smoke stopper: SHORT detected", toast "The smoke stopper lamp lit up: something is shorted. The cap is in backwards: flip it before you plug in for real" (buzz, 3.8 s) and leave the smoke check failed. | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L372-374 | must | e2e |
| DLA-143 | With the cap the right way round the smoke stopper test must pass, log "smoke stopper: no short, current ok" and toast "No short: the lamp only blinked. Safe to plug in" (ding). | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L374-375 | must | e2e |
| DLA-144 | "Check motor direction" in flight must answer "Land first", and on a craft with no motors "Nothing to spin on this craft". | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L378 | must | e2e |
| DLA-145 | "Check motor direction" without the motor wires and FC fitted must answer "Wire the motors and mount the flight controller first". | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L378-379 | must | e2e |
| DLA-146 | "Check motor direction" with the props on must be refused with "PROPS OFF! Never spin motors on the bench with props on" for 2.8 s. | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L379-380; SPEC.md L116 | must | e2e |
| DLA-147 | "Check motor direction" unplugged with no battery fitted must answer "Plug in a battery (USB alone can’t spin motors)". | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L380-381 | must | e2e |
| DLA-148 | The motor check must log "$ motors tab: spin M1, M2, M3, M4 one at a time" (listing the craft's own motors) and spin each motor in turn for 1.2 s. | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L382-383 | must | e2e |
| DLA-149 | While the motor check runs the other part tags must hide, and the spinning motor must show a 288° arrow (radius 22·u at the arrow height) with a pill "{M} CW" or "{M} CCW". | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L383-384 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-150 | A reversed motor must show the opposite direction in red with " WRONG" appended (for example "M3 CW WRONG"). | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L384-385 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-151 | When a motor was reversed, the check must pass, log "{M} direction reversed in ESC config" and toast "{M} spun the wrong way: reversed it in the ESC configurator. They all spin right now" (ding, 3.2 s). | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L386-387 | must | e2e |
| DLA-152 | When no motor was reversed the check must toast "Every motor spins the right way" (ding). | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L388 | must | e2e |
| DLA-153 | The battery must stay plugged after the motor check. | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L388 | must | e2e |
| DLA-154 | Calibrating in flight must answer "Land first", and without the FC fitted "Mount the flight controller first". | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L390-391 | must | e2e |
| DLA-155 | Calibrating with the FC mounted backwards must pass but log "acc calibrated (but board_align is wrong!)" and toast "Calibrated level. Note: the arrow on the FC points backwards..." (tick). | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L391-392 | must | e2e |
| DLA-156 | A normal calibration must log "$ calibrate accelerometer: done, level = 0.0 / 0.0" and toast "Accelerometer calibrated on a flat surface" (ding). | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L392-393 | must | e2e |
| DLA-157 | Accelerometer calibration must work with the battery unplugged. | drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L394 | must | e2e |

## DLA · Real mistakes, bench drawing, messages, wiring, lesson mechanics

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DLA-158 | Choosing "FLIGHT CTRL: arrow backward" (whoop: "AIO BOARD: ...") must make the craft flip on take-off (armed > 0.25 s and thrust > 5 %: roll rate +900 °/s, pitch rate +500 °/s) and crash with "Flipped on take-off: the flight controller is mounted the wrong way round". | drone-lab-a.md §4.5 L405 | must | e2e |
| DLA-159 | Choosing "XT60 + CAP: stripe to plus" must be fixable only by unplugging, re-adding "stripe to minus" and re-running the smoke stopper test. | drone-lab-a.md §4.5 L406 | must | e2e |
| DLA-160 | With the cap fault present, flying must be refused with "Fix the fault first (unplug, replace the capacitor)". | drone-lab-a.md §4.5 L406; §4.7 L434 | must | e2e |
| DLA-161 | Flying after fitting the XT60 without a passed smoke stopper test must be refused with "Do the smoke stopper test first: a short on the first plug kills ESCs" (2.6 s). | drone-lab-a.md §4.5 L407; §4.7 L435 | must | e2e |
| DLA-162 | Choosing "PROPS: swapped CW/CCW" must make the craft flip the other way on take-off and crash with "Flipped on take-off: the props are on the wrong motors". | drone-lab-a.md §4.5 L411 | must | e2e |
| DLA-163 | Placing or re-orienting the FC must leave arming refused with "Arming blocked: ACC not calibrated. Run Calibrate accelerometer on the bench" until calibration is run. | drone-lab-a.md §4.5 L412 | must | e2e |
| DLA-164 | Arming in a manual-throttle mode with throttle above 8 % must be refused with "Arming blocked: throttle not at zero (that is a safety rule, not a bug)". | drone-lab-a.md §4.5 L413 | must | e2e |
| DLA-165 | The alt-hold climb must be limited by the design's thrust, so a build below about T:W 1.7 climbs slower than 2.2 m/s ((g + 3·2.2)/(tw·g) ≤ 1 is the original condition for the full rate). | drone-lab-a.md §4.5 L416 | must | unit |
| DLA-166 | When several build faults exist, take-off must report them in the order FC reversed, then props swapped, then motor direction not checked. | drone-lab-a.md §4.5 L419 | must | unit |

| DLA-169 | The selected part's pill must be drawn in the accent colour. | drone-lab-a.md §4.6 L425 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-170 | Props must draw as blades when slow and as a translucent disc when rpm > 0.12 (opacity quad5 0.18+0.22·rpm; whoop and cinewhoop 0.2+0.25·rpm; long range 0.16+0.2·rpm). | drone-lab-a.md §4.6 L426-428 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-171 | In flight, armed props must spin at rpm = max(0.2, effective throttle). | drone-lab-a.md §4.6 L428 | must | unit |

| DLA-173 | Asking to fly an unfinished craft must answer "Finish building it first: Build it for me is fine" (2.4 s). | drone-lab-a.md §4.7 L434 | must | e2e |
| DLA-174 | "Back to the bench" while already on the bench must answer "Already on the bench" (tick, 1.2 s). | drone-lab-a.md §4.7 L435 | must | e2e |
| DLA-175 | Asking for FPV on the bench must answer "Pick a flight mode first, then switch to FPV". | drone-lab-a.md §4.7 L436 | must | e2e |
| DLA-176 | The wind chip must toggle gusty wind with "Gusty wind on" / "Wind off" (click, 1.4 s). | drone-lab-a.md §4.7 L437 | must | e2e |
| DLA-177 | Arming before a flight mode is picked must answer "Pick a flight mode under Fly first". | drone-lab-a.md §4.7 L437 | must | e2e |

| DLA-181 | Leaving the field must show "Out of the field: turn back (R resets to the pad)". | drone-lab-a.md §4.7 L450 | must | e2e |
| DLA-182 | After a crash the flight message must read "{reason}. Press R to reset". | drone-lab-a.md §4.7 L452 | must | e2e |
| DLA-183 | The crash reasons must include "Crashed into the ground: props broken", the three "Flipped on take-off: ..." texts, "Hit an obstacle", "Hit a tree" and "Hit a roof", each used only for that kind of contact. | drone-lab-a.md §4.7 L456-457; §10 defect 13 | must | e2e |
| DLA-184 | The wiring diagram must be generated from the craft's wiring data (nodes, lines, line labels, legend) on the original's 760 × 340 layout. | drone-lab-a.md §5 L463; §2 L228 | must | unit |
| DLA-185 | The wiring diagram must use the hand-inked Colorfigures look (ink filter, wobble, paper grain, the five palettes) and the same part data as the 3D figure. | SPEC.md L16, L154; colorfigures-guide.md §3.5 L894-898 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-186 | Each wiring node must be a 150 × 56 rounded box (rx 10) on paper with an 8 px colour bar on the left, a bold title and a muted sub line. | drone-lab-a.md §5 L464-465 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-187 | Wiring lines must be solid for power and motor phases and dashed (6 4) for data, signal or RF, with small muted labels. | drone-lab-a.md §5 L466-467 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-188 | The wiring legend must default to power #e2483d, data / signal #43a35a, video #f2c230 and motor phases #f08a2b, one entry per 150 px. | drone-lab-a.md §5 L468-469 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-189 | Every wiring node must be pressable by pointer and keyboard and select the part with the same id (tick): the block turns accent, the part's pill lights on the bench, and "Selected part" shows it. | drone-lab-a.md §5 L470-472; SPEC.md L115, L129 | must | e2e |
| DLA-190 | Pressing the selected part again (list button, wiring block or figure) must deselect it. | drone-lab-a.md §6.4 L493 | must | e2e |
| DLA-191 | The wiring diagram must show the chosen battery's name, the chosen motor's name and the nominal pack voltage {V.toFixed(1)} V (22.2, 14.8, 3.7, 7.4 V for the defaults), updating when the design changes. | drone-lab-a.md §5 L473-474 | must | e2e |
| DLA-192 | In the range mode the CLI card must show the range's own code or "# fly the checkpoints in order; the run time is kept". | drone-lab-a.md §6.1 L482-483 | must | e2e |
| DLA-193 | The log terminal should use background #1d2a26 and text #8af0ae and keep only the last 10 lines. | drone-lab-a.md §6.2 L486 | should | screenshot |
| DLA-194 | Each quiz question must render as "{i}. {question}" followed by one chip per answer; the right chip turns green with chime, a wrong chip is struck through and faded with buzz, and the learner may try again. | drone-lab-a.md §6.3 L489-490 | must | e2e |
| DLA-195 | Quiz answers and a per-craft quiz score must persist in localStorage across reloads and craft switches (the original kept none). | drone-lab-a.md §6.3 L490; SPEC.md L66, L126 | must | e2e |

## DLA · Entering flight, modes, multirotor physics, battery, controls

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DLA-196 | Entering flight must be refused, in this order, for an unfinished craft, a present fault, or a smoke test not passed (unless the craft has no smoke test), each with its message. | drone-lab-a.md §7.1 L501-504 | must | unit |
| DLA-197 | Entering flight must set the mode, switch to the flight view, plug the battery in, close the exploded view, clear any autopilot, park the sticks, log "-- field: {mode title} --" and play whoosh. | drone-lab-a.md §7.1 L506-509 | must | e2e |
| DLA-198 | Switching mode while airborne and not crashed must keep the same flight (position, velocity, battery state); otherwise a new flight starts on the pad. | drone-lab-a.md §7.1 L506-507 | must | e2e |
| DLA-199 | "Back to the bench" must disarm, stop the motor hum, leave the flight and FPV views, and remember the FPV choice for the next flight. | drone-lab-a.md §7.1 L509-510 | must | e2e |
| DLA-200 | A new flight must start on the pad at z = 0 with all motion zero, pack voltage cells × 4.2, 0 mAh used and the calculator values of the current design. | drone-lab-a.md §7.1 L512-513 | must | unit |
| DLA-201 | R and the Reset button must start a new flight (keeping the camera), zero and park both the keyboard and virtual sticks, and play whoosh. | drone-lab-a.md §7.1 L513-514 | must | e2e |
| DLA-202 | The default flight modes must be titled "Altitude hold (easy)", "Angle mode", "Acro (rate) mode" and "Gate race", plus "Range: {range title}" when the craft has a range. | drone-lab-a.md §7.2 L518-526 | must | e2e |
| DLA-203 | Altitude hold must self-level and treat the throttle stick as a climb-rate command. | drone-lab-a.md §7.2 L522 | must | unit |
| DLA-204 | Angle mode must self-level with a manual throttle. | drone-lab-a.md §7.2 L523; SPEC.md L117 | must | unit |
| DLA-205 | Acro mode must treat the sticks as rotation rates with a manual throttle and no self-levelling. | drone-lab-a.md §7.2 L524; SPEC.md L117 | must | unit |
| DLA-206 | Gate race must fly like angle mode and add gate timing. | drone-lab-a.md §7.2 L525; SPEC.md L117 | must | unit |
| DLA-207 | The range mode must use altitude hold unless the range sets alt to false, and must run the checkpoints. | drone-lab-a.md §7.2 L526 | must | unit |
| DLA-208 | A craft's own mode must be able to declare altitude-hold or acro stick handling. | drone-lab-a.md §7.2 L528 | must | unit |
| DLA-209 | Flight physics must step in substeps of at most 10 ms (n = ceil(dt/0.01)) whatever the frame rate. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L531 | must | unit |
| DLA-210 | When disarmed or crashed the effective throttle must spin down at 3 per second. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L533 | must | unit |
| DLA-211 | On the ground (z ≤ 0.02 and effective throttle < 0.3) roll and pitch must decay by ×0.8 per substep. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L536 | must | unit |
| DLA-212 | In acro, roll and pitch rates must be stick × 540 °/s. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L537 | must | unit |
| DLA-213 | In self-levelling modes the target angle must be stick × 38°, approached at min(1, 9·dt) per substep (time constant about 0.11 s). | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L538 | must | unit |
| DLA-214 | In every mode the yaw rate must be −stick.yaw × 200 °/s. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L539 | must | unit |
| DLA-215 | Altitude hold must command vz = stick.thr × 2.2 m/s with need = (g + 3·(target − vz)) / (tw·g) / max(0.4, cos roll · cos pitch) and throttle = clamp(sqrt(max(0, need)), 0, 1), and throttle 0 on the ground with the stick at or below zero. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L543-545 | must | unit |
| DLA-216 | In manual-throttle modes the throttle must be clamp(thrAbs, 0, 1). | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L546 | must | unit |
| DLA-217 | Motor spool-up must follow thrEff += (thr² − thrEff) · min(1, 18·dt) (about 55 ms lag), with thrEff the fraction of maximum thrust. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L547 | must | unit |
| DLA-218 | A build fault must act once armed for more than 0.25 s with thrEff > 0.05: roll rate +900 °/s (−900 °/s for props swapped or motor not checked) and pitch rate +500 °/s. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L549-550 | must | unit |
| DLA-219 | An over-KV design must build heat at dt·thrEff·1.6 and desync when heat passes 9, which for 2450 KV on 6S is about 5.6 s at full throttle and about 140 s at hover. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L550-553; Appendix B L1434 | must | unit |
| DLA-220 | A desync must show "Desync! {kv}KV on {cells}S overheats: the ESC lost a motor", play pop and log "ESC desync: motor {n} overtemp" naming the motor that actually failed. | drone-lab-a.md §4.7 L445, L452; §7.3 L550 | must | e2e |
| DLA-221 | Thrust must be thrEff · n · thrust/1000 · g newtons, scaled by max(0, 1 − (z − CEIL + 3)/3) above CEIL − 3 (soft ceiling, CEIL defaults to 12 m). | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L557-558 | must | unit |
| DLA-222 | The thrust vector must follow the craft frame built from yaw, pitch and −roll, so +pitch tilts thrust toward the nose and +roll toward the left. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L559 | must | unit |
| DLA-223 | Acceleration must be up · T/m − drag · (v − wind) · (abs(v) + 1) with g subtracted on z, integrated by semi-implicit Euler (drag default 0.045). | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L561-562 | must | unit |
| DLA-224 | Holding W in altitude hold must climb at 2.2 m/s and settle near CEIL − 1.79 m (quad5 10.2 m, peak 10.47 m); released it must sink to about CEIL − 3. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L564-565; Appendix B L1429 | must | unit |
| DLA-225 | The 5-inch must reach about 14 m/s (50 km/h) at full forward stick in angle mode and about 50 m/s in an acro dive. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L566-567; Appendix B L1431-1432 | must | unit |
| DLA-226 | Touching the ground must crash the craft when t > 0.3 s and (vz < −3.2 m/s, or tilt > 55° with speed > 1.5 m/s, or speed > 6 m/s), with the build-fault reason or "Crashed into the ground: props broken". | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L569-570 | must | unit |
| DLA-227 | A soft ground contact must set z = 0, vz = max(0, vz), scale horizontal speed by 0.8 and level the craft when not crashed and thrEff < 0.3. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L570-571 | must | unit |
| DLA-228 | Leaving the field (x or y outside [−2, FIELD+2] m) must show the out-of-field message once per excursion. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L573-574 | must | e2e |
| DLA-229 | A crashed craft should tumble on screen (tumble +8/s, roll +6/s). | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L574-575 | should | screenshot |
| DLA-230 | Drawn props should turn by dt·(6 + 60·thrEff) while armed. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L575 | should | unit |
| DLA-231 | A crash must disarm, add one to the crash count, show "{reason}. Press R to reset", play pop then sizzle 100 ms later, log "CRASH: {reason, lowercased}" and remove the broken props. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L577-578 | must | e2e |
| DLA-232 | Battery current while armed must be hoverA · (thrEff / max(0.05, hoverThr²))^1.25 + 0.4 A idle (FC, VTX). | drone-lab-a.md §7.4 L585 | must | unit |
| DLA-233 | Charge used must be integrated as used += amps · dt / 3.6 mAh, with state of charge max(0, 1 − used/mAh). | drone-lab-a.md §7.4 L586-587 | must | unit |
| DLA-234 | A LiPo pack's resting voltage must follow cells · (3.3 + 0.9 · soc^0.7) (4.2 V per cell full) or a documented better curve, with load sag subtracted. | drone-lab-a.md §7.4 L588 | must | unit |
| DLA-235 | Falling below 3.5 V per cell must show "LOW BATTERY: land now (3.5 V per cell)" once, with the error effect. | drone-lab-a.md §7.4 L590-591 | must | e2e |
| DLA-236 | Falling below 3.2 V per cell must disarm with "Battery empty: the ESCs cut out. Never fly a LiPo this low" and the unplug effect. | drone-lab-a.md §7.4 L591 | must | e2e |
| DLA-237 | Flight keys must work only in flight and never while the learner is typing in an input, select or textarea. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L597 | must | e2e |
| DLA-238 | Flight keys (W S A D, arrows, Space, R, V) must call preventDefault only while flying, so Space still presses a focused button and arrows still scroll the page otherwise. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L597-598; current-simlab-audit.md §3.8 FS-02 | must | e2e |
| DLA-239 | Space (ignoring key repeat) must arm and disarm, R must reset to the pad and V must toggle FPV, each acting on the keydown event itself. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L599; current-simlab-audit.md §3.8 FS-03 | must | e2e |
| DLA-240 | Held keys must map yaw = D − A, pitch = Up − Down, roll = Left − Right (left arrow banks left), each smoothed toward its target at min(1, 10·dt) per frame. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L600-601 | must | unit |
| DLA-241 | Arrow Up (forward pitch) must move the craft forward along its nose. | drone-lab-a.md §7.3 L559; current-simlab-audit.md §3.8 FS-01 | must | e2e |
| DLA-242 | Virtual-stick values must override the keyboard while a stick is held. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L601 | must | unit |
| DLA-243 | In altitude-hold modes W/S must set the climb command directly and spring to 0 on release; in manual modes they must move the throttle at 0.32 per second (0 to 100 % in about 3.1 s) and leave it where it is. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L602-603 | must | unit |
| DLA-244 | While an autopilot flies, the stick inputs it owns must come from the autopilot, not the keys. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L604 | must | unit |
| DLA-245 | The T key must do nothing except on crafts that use it (the VTOL transition). | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L605 | must | e2e |
| DLA-246 | Disarming must work in the air too (the craft then falls), play unplug and log "disarmed". | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L607-608 | must | e2e |
| DLA-247 | Arming while disarmed in the air (z > 0.05 m) must be ignored. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L608 | must | e2e |
| DLA-248 | A successful arm must reset the flight clock, play power, log "ARMED  mode: {mode title}" and show the mode's arming hint. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L609; §4.7 L444 | must | e2e |
| DLA-249 | Two virtual sticks of 118 px must show only in flight, left "THROTTLE · YAW" and right "PITCH · ROLL", with a 38 px knob travelling 40 px clamped to the unit circle. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L611-612; SPEC.md L117 | must | e2e |
| DLA-250 | Each virtual stick must capture its own pointer by id and release on pointerup, pointercancel or loss of capture. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L612; current-simlab-audit.md §3.8 FS-07 | must | e2e |
| DLA-251 | The left stick's x must be yaw; its y must be the climb command (spring back) in altitude-hold modes and absolute throttle (y+1)/2 with no spring in manual modes, the touch starting from the knob's current place. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L612-614 | must | e2e |
| DLA-252 | The right stick must set roll = −x and pitch = y and spring back to centre. | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L614 | must | e2e |
| DLA-253 | Parking must centre both knobs, except a manual-mode throttle knob, which parks at the bottom (zero). | drone-lab-a.md §7.5 L614-615 | must | e2e |
| DLA-254 | On touch screens every keyboard-only flight action (arm, reset, FPV, mode change, and T on the VTOL) must also be reachable by an on-screen control. | drone-lab-a.md §1.4 L121-122; §7.5 L599; SPEC.md L128 | must | e2e |

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DLA-255 | A range must be data with title, brief, field, pad, ground colour, alt flag, camTilt, keepGates, code, prims and goals. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L619 | must | unit |
| DLA-256 | The range must support box prims (x, y, z, w, d, h, yaw, colour, solid by default) drawn as oriented boxes. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L625 | must | unit |
| DLA-257 | The range must support house prims: a box with a 4-sided pyramid roof (radius diagonal/2 × 1.02, height roof or 0.45·min(w, d), turned yaw + 45°, colour #c96a4e by default). | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L626 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-258 | The range must support cyl prims (x, y, z, r, h, top radius r1, 14 sides by default, rotation, colour, solid) drawn as cylinders or cones. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L627 | must | unit |
| DLA-259 | A tree prim (default height 3 m) must be an 8-sided trunk (r 0.16, height 0.35h, #bf8a4f) under a 12-sided crown cone (base radius max(0.5, 0.22h) at 0.3h, height 0.7h, top radius 0.03, #6aa86b). | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L628 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-260 | A pole prim must be an 8-sided cylinder (radius 0.08 m, colour #8a8f93 by default). | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L629 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-261 | A gate prim must be two 0.07 m posts of height h + 0.07 at ±(w/2 + 0.035) across the heading plus a top bar (colour #ece6d6 by default). | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L630 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-262 | The range must support flat and disc ground decals with optional labels. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L631-632 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-263 | A craft with no range prims must get the default 6 trees and 5 gates; with prims the race gates must be added as solids only when keepGates is set. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L634-635 | must | unit |
| DLA-264 | Every field must show a white pad disc (radius 0.6 m) labelled "H" at the pad. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L635 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-265 | Collisions must be tested every physics step with the craft's collision radius, skipping solids whose height span grown by that radius does not contain the craft. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L637-638 | must | unit |
| DLA-266 | Box solids must collide when the craft is inside the rotated rectangle grown by the collision radius. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L638 | must | unit |
| DLA-267 | Cylinder and cone solids must collide using the radius interpolated at the craft's height, with a diamond test (abs(lx) + abs(ly) < r + m) for 4-sided roofs and a circle test for round solids. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L639-641 | must | unit |
| DLA-268 | Range checkpoints must count only in range mode and only in order. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L643 | must | unit |
| DLA-269 | A checkpoint with a heading must be a ring, passed when the signed distance along its normal crosses from below 0 to 0 or more with hypot(lateral, dz) < r. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L643-645 | must | unit |
| DLA-270 | A checkpoint without a heading must be a sphere, touched when the 3D distance is below r. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L645 | must | unit |
| DLA-271 | Each checkpoint passed must play chime and show "{label} done" (or "Checkpoint {i}/{N}" when it has no label). | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L646; §4.7 L453 | must | e2e |
| DLA-272 | Finishing the last checkpoint must record the run time, count a run, keep the best, show "{title} done: {t.toFixed(1)} s" (+ " (best!)"), log "range run {t} s", play ding and restart the course at checkpoint 1. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L646-648 | must | e2e |
| DLA-273 | Race gates must count only in race mode, passed by crossing the gate plane along (cos h, sin h) with lateral offset < gate width/2 and 0.05 m < z < gate height. | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L650-651 | must | unit |
| DLA-274 | Passing a gate must play chime. | drone-lab-a.md §8 L717 | must | e2e |
| DLA-275 | Passing the first gate must start the lap clock; passing the last must show "Lap {t.toFixed(2)} s" (+ " (best!)"), log "lap {t} s", play ding, count the lap, update the best and restart at gate 1 with the clock running (flying laps). | drone-lab-a.md §7.6 L651-653 | must | e2e |
| DLA-276 | The iso flight view must be flyVB mm wide (default 5200; × 0.8 on phones) and 0.75 × as tall as wide (1.05 × on phones). | drone-lab-a.md §7.7 L657-658 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-277 | The iso flight view must keep ink weight and the hand-drawn filter consistent at every zoom, with the filter region pinned to the visible frame so WebKit does not drop it. | drone-lab-a.md §7.7 L658-659 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-278 | The iso camera must follow the craft at (x, y, z·camZ) with smoothing. | drone-lab-a.md §7.7 L660 | must | e2e |
| DLA-279 | The field must be a 40 mm slab with a grid every max(1, round(FIELD/30)) m. | drone-lab-a.md §7.7 L660-661 | must | screenshot |

| DLA-282 | The craft must be drawn flyScale × life size in the iso view, with a dashed height line and a ground dot when z > 0.05 m. | drone-lab-a.md §7.7 L663-664 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-283 | The iso view must tag the next gate "GATE {n}" or the next checkpoint with its label or "CHECKPOINT {n}". | drone-lab-a.md §7.7 L664-665 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-284 | The FPV view must be a 1000 × 750 view whose goggles image is the rounded rectangle (250, 0, 750 × 562, rx 26), leaving the button column clear on desktop. | drone-lab-a.md §7.8 L671-672 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-285 | The FPV camera must sit 0.08 m above the craft with its yaw, pitch and roll (plus tumble), tilted up by the range's camTilt, else the craft's, else 25°; gimbal crafts must use yaw only. | drone-lab-a.md §7.8 L672-674 | must | unit |
| DLA-286 | The FPV projection must use the craft's horizontal FOV (default 105°), focal length 375/tan(fov/2) px and a 0.06 m near plane with polygon clipping. | drone-lab-a.md §7.8 L674 | must | unit |
| DLA-287 | The FPV scene must draw sky #cfe2ea, 28 hills (#b8c7c3, heights 25 + 45·abs(sin 1.7i) m) on a ring of radius max(500, 4·FIELD) m, a ±3000 m ground plane #e3e6d2 and the field square in the range's ground colour with a grid. | drone-lab-a.md §7.8 L675-677 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-288 | FPV solids must be back-face culled, depth sorted and shaded by SUN = norm(−0.45, −0.25, 0.86) (light tone above 0.55, mid above 0.05, else dark), with decals drawn far to near and cylinders as side strips plus a top cap when seen from above. | drone-lab-a.md §7.8 L677-678 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-289 | FPV checkpoint rings must have width clamp(260/dist, 1.5, 18), the next one in the accent colour and the others #f4f1e6. | drone-lab-a.md §7.8 L679 | must | screenshot |

| DLA-301 | While armed a motor hum must play: sawtooth plus square (×2.01) through a 1400 Hz low-pass at 95 + 430·thrEff Hz with gain 0.025 + 0.05·thrEff. | drone-lab-a.md §7.9 L698-699 | must | unit |
| DLA-302 | All sound must be synthesized with WebAudio from code (no audio files): a compressor at the output, a music bus through a 2400 Hz low-pass and an effects bus at 0.55 gain. | drone-lab-a.md §8 L709-710 | must | review |
| DLA-303 | "Lofi" must play a 74 bpm loop Dm9 - G13 - Cmaj9 - Am9 (rhodes-like chords, bass, kick, soft snare, hats, sparse melody, vinyl crackle) that fades in and out, and must be off by default. | drone-lab-a.md §8 L711-712; SPEC.md L55 | must | e2e |
| DLA-304 | "Effects" must be on by default. | drone-lab-a.md §8 L713 | must | e2e |
| DLA-305 | Each effect must fire on the original's events: tick (buttons, selections), place (part lands), whoosh (add, strip, build, explode, fly, bench, reset), click (select change, FPV, smoke test start, wind), plug, power, unplug, pop + sizzle (cap vents, crash), pop (desync), buzz (short, wrong answer, compass warning), ding (test passed, lap, run done, landed), chime (gate, checkpoint, right answer), error (refusals, low battery, link lost). | drone-lab-a.md §8 L714-718 | must | e2e |
| DLA-306 | SimLab should not send usage-tracking beacons or keep a visitor id (the original posted board, btn, pick, part, quiz, fault and mastered events to /api/track). | drone-lab-a.md §8 L720-723; colorfigures-guide.md §3.1 L596, L628 | should | review |
| DLA-307 | Per-craft progress must persist in localStorage: build finished, tests passed, quiz answers, best lap and best run per course, crash count, and the "mastered" events (lap, range run, rescue landing). | drone-lab-a.md §8 L723-726; SPEC.md L66, L126 | must | e2e |

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DLA-308 | The 5-inch freestyle quad must match or exceed the original Fig. 01 in every aspect listed below (design, build, tests, wiring, modes, CLI, range, quiz, practice, drawing). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L735-854; SPEC.md L58-63, L109 | must | review |
| DLA-309 | quad5 must be named "5-inch freestyle quad" with sub "Betaflight + FPV", hint "design it, build it, smoke-test it, then fly it", palette tomato, mat 280, benchH 120, flyScale 4, flyVB 8000, ceiling 12 m, drag 0.045, hitR 0.14, FPV tilt 25° and prop blur on. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L737-739 | must | unit |
| DLA-310 | All Betaflight crafts must number motors M1 rear-right CW, M2 front-right CCW, M3 rear-left CCW, M4 front-left CW ("props in"). | drone-lab-a.md §9 L732-733 | must | unit |
| DLA-311 | quad5 batteries must be "6S 1100 mAh" (6S, 1100 mAh, 178 g, $38, #2b2a28, label "6S 1100"), "6S 1800 mAh" (282 g, $54, #2f5fa8) and "4S 1500 mAh" (4S, 182 g, $30, #d9a520). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L744-748 | must | unit |
| DLA-312 | quad5 motors must be "2306 1750KV" (32 g, $22), "2207 1950KV" (34 g, $24) and "2306 2450KV" (31 g, $20). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L750-751 | must | unit |
| DLA-313 | quad5 props must be "5x4.3x3 tri-blade" (pf 1.00, eff 1.00, 4.4 g, $4), "5.1x2.5x3 (efficient)" (pf 0.84, eff 1.18, 4.0 g, $4) and "5x5x3 (aggressive)" (pf 1.10, eff 0.88, 4.8 g, $4). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L751-752 | must | unit |
| DLA-314 | quad5 fixed parts must be the 5" carbon frame (118 g, $39, M), 45A 4-in-1 ESC (8 g, $45, E), F7 flight controller (7 g, $39, E), analog FPV camera (7 g, $25, E), 5.8 GHz VTX + antenna (9 g, $30, E), ELRS 2.4 GHz receiver (2 g, $18, E), XT60 pigtail + low-ESR cap (12 g, $4, E), top plate + standoffs + screws (22 g, $8, M) and battery strap + pad (5 g, $4, M). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L754-756 | must | unit |
| DLA-315 | The quad5 default design must show 514 g, 1600 g per motor, 12.5:1, hover 28 %, 144 A, 12.3 min hover, 5.2 min aggressive and $287 + $59 = $346. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L757 | must | unit |
| DLA-316 | quad5 must build from 12 parts with these prerequisites: FRAME; MOTORS [FRAME]; ESC [FRAME]; MOTOR WIRES [MOTORS, ESC]; FLIGHT CTRL [ESC]; XT60 + CAP [ESC]; CAMERA [FRAME]; RECEIVER [FC]; VTX [FC]; TOP PLATE [FC, CAM, RX, VTX]; BATTERY [TOP, XT60]; PROPS [MOTORS, TOP]. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L759-771 | must | unit |
| DLA-317 | The quad5 build must allow the brief's order frame → motors → ESC → wiring → FC → VTX → camera → receiver → battery → props. | SPEC.md L113; drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L759-771 | must | e2e |
| DLA-318 | quad5's polar parts must offer FLIGHT CTRL "arrow forward" / "arrow backward", XT60 + CAP "stripe to minus" / "stripe to plus" and PROPS "props in (correct)" / "swapped CW/CCW". | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L764-771 | must | e2e |
| DLA-319 | The quad5 part texts must teach: True-X, 5 mm arms, 225 mm motor to motor and carbon conducts; outrunner bells, KV × volts = prop speed; four ESCs on one board, DC to 3-phase; three motor wires in any order, direction set in software; gyro + PID, arrow to the nose. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L760-764 | must | review |
| DLA-320 | The quad5 part texts must teach: 1000 µF spike cap, stripe to minus or it vents; camera tilted up about 30°; ELRS on an FC UART with two antennas at 90°; never power a VTX without an antenna; standoff height matters; never below 3.5 V/cell, store at 3.8 V, never charge unattended; two CW and two CCW props cancel torque, fitted last. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L765-771 | must | review |
| DLA-321 | quad5 motors must sit at ±79.5 mm on both axes, and the bench must tag FC, ESC, CAM, VTX, XT60, MOTORS and BATT. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L773 | must | unit |
| DLA-322 | quad5 wiring nodes must be BATTERY (battery name), XT60 + CAP "1000 uF 35 V", 4-IN-1 ESC "45 A x 4, BLHeli_32", FLIGHT CTRL "F7, gyro, Betaflight", RECEIVER "ELRS 2.4 GHz", CAMERA "analog, 30 deg", VTX "5.8 GHz, 400 mW" and MOTORS x4 (motor name). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L775-777 | must | e2e |
| DLA-323 | quad5 wiring lines must run battery → XT60 → ESC (red/black with the voltage label), ESC → motors (3 orange, "3-phase x4"), ESC → FC ("VBAT" red, "M1-M4" dashed green), FC → RX ("UART2 + 5V"), FC → camera ("video in", yellow) and FC → VTX ("video + SmartAudio", yellow + dashed green). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L777-779 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-324 | The quad5 overview must present the standard first FPV build and link the FC's PID loop to the Robotics Lab line follower (same idea, three axes). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L781-782; SPEC.md L66 | must | review |
| DLA-325 | The quad5 build code must keep resource, set align_board_yaw = 0 (180 if the arrow points back), set motor_pwm_protocol = DSHOT600, set yaw_motors_reversed = OFF ("props in"), serial 1 64 115200 57600 0 115200 (UART2 = ELRS) and save. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L784-793 | must | unit |
| DLA-326 | The quad5 build code should also set serialrx_provider = CRSF so the ELRS receiver setup is complete. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L791; SPEC.md L118 | should | review |
| DLA-327 | must not copy: the alt-hold code "set alt_hold_throttle_deadband = 15" is not a Betaflight setting and "aux 1 ANGLE ..." is not CLI syntax; the alt-hold snippet must use real names for the chosen Betaflight version (4.6+ altitude hold) and numeric aux mode ids, or say it is a Modes-tab step. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L796-800; §10 defect 25 | must | review |
| DLA-328 | must not copy: "set p_level = 50" / "set i_level = 50" are pre-4.5 names; the angle snippet must use angle_p_gain (default 50) and keep angle_limit = 38 to match the sim's 38°. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L801-805 | must | review |
| DLA-329 | must not copy: "roll_rc_rate = 100, roll_srate = 70 # ~540 deg/s" is wrong (about 667 °/s with BETAFLIGHT rates, 700 °/s max with the default ACTUAL rates); the acro snippet must name its rates_type and give values whose full-stick rate equals the sim's 540 °/s (ACTUAL: roll_srate = 54). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L806-811; drone-lab-b.md §3 L3300 | must | review |
| DLA-330 | must not copy: "set osd_timer_1 = ON" and "set osd_stat_max_spd = ON" are not Betaflight 4.5+ settings; the race snippet must use osd_tim1 (packed value), osd_tim_1_pos (element position) and osd_stat_bitmask. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L812-815; drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L475-476 | must | review |
| DLA-331 | The quad5 range "Freestyle bando" must have brief "Thread the gap, fly through the bando, top the tower, dive it, land", a 40 m field, pad (20, 6), ground #dde3cc, no race gates and altitude-hold controls. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L820-821 | must | unit |
| DLA-332 | The bando range must hold the road and slab decals, six 0.6 × 0.6 × 6.3 m pillars, floors at 3 m and 6 m, the back wall, the gap (two 0.4 × 6 × 4 m walls 1.2 m apart, #e0a26a), the 10 m dive tower with its top slab, five 8 m power poles, five trees and two barrels at the listed places. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L822-826 | must | unit |
| DLA-333 | The bando checkpoints must be THE GAP (15.8, 20, 2, r 0.8, ring 90°), BANDO (30, 22, 1.5, r 1.1, ring 90°), TOWER TOP (8, 30, 11.6, r 1.6, sphere), DIVE (8, 27.4, 1.6, r 1.3, ring −90°) and LAND (20, 6, 0.8, r 1.6, sphere), in that order. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L828-834 | must | unit |
| DLA-334 | The quad5 race must use the 5 gates [18.5, 15, 0], [21.5, 18.5, 70], [18, 21.5, 165], [13, 20, 215], [12, 15.5, −60] of 1.6 m. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L836 | must | unit |
| DLA-335 | quad5 quiz 1 must ask "Why do the props go on last?" with the right answer "A motor test or short with props on can throw the quad at your face" (others: "They are fragile", "They block the camera"). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L841 | must | e2e |
| DLA-336 | quad5 quiz 2 must ask "The arrow on the flight controller points backwards. What happens on takeoff?" with the right answer "It corrects the wrong way and flips instantly". | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L842 | must | e2e |
| DLA-337 | quad5 quiz 3 must ask "What does the big capacitor on the XT60 lead do?" with the right answer "Soaks up voltage spikes from the ESCs". | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L843 | must | e2e |
| DLA-338 | quad5 quiz 4 must ask "You let go of the sticks in acro mode. The quad..." with the right answer "keeps whatever angle it had". | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L844 | must | e2e |
| DLA-339 | The quad5 practice task must ask for five gate laps in angle mode then one in acro, and for a real build: smoke-stopper the first plug, check motor direction with props off, compare the real flight time with the estimate. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L846-847 | must | review |
| DLA-340 | quad5 must draw a 112 × 46 mm carbon plate with four 96 mm arms, a green ESC with four FETs, and a blue FC with a white direction arrow and a blue LED when powered. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L849-850 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-341 | quad5 must draw a yellow XT60, red/black leads and a blue capacitor whose white stripe swaps side with the orientation (brown when popped). | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L850-851 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-342 | quad5 must draw the camera between two palette-coloured plates with the lens tilted 60° from vertical, the RX with two whips and the VTX with an angled antenna and a red LED. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L851-852 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-343 | quad5 must draw the top plate on 30 mm standoffs, a battery box in the pack colour with its label (longer for packs over 1500 mAh), the battery lead only when plugged, and tri-blade props of radius 63 mm. | drone-lab-a.md §9.1 L852-854 | must | screenshot |

| DLA-354 | The whoop part texts must teach: plug-in motor connectors with direction set in Betaflight; a 14 mm nano camera with about 20° uptilt; only the receiver's wire antenna sticks out; the 25-400 mW VTX is on the AIO and 25 mW is plenty indoors; press-fit 4-blade props go on last, after the motor check. | drone-lab-a.md §9.2 L878-883 | must | review |

| DLA-368 | The whoop build code must keep set motor_pwm_protocol = DSHOT300, set dshot_bidir = ON (RPM filter), set yaw_motors_reversed = OFF (props in; ON for props out), set vbat_warning_cell_voltage = 330, aux 2 35 1 1700 2100 0 0 (turtle mode switch) and save. | drone-lab-a.md §9.2 L908-917 | must | unit |

| DLA-386 | cinewhoop fixed parts must be the 3" frame + plates (62 g, $55, M), printed ducts + bumpers (52 g, $0, M), 35A 4-in-1 ESC 20×20 (7 g, $40, E), F7 FC 20×20 (6 g, $40, E), O3-style air unit + camera (36 g, $229, E), ELRS receiver (2 g, $18, E), XT60 + cap (10 g, $4, E), top plate + standoffs (12 g, $6, M), strap + pad (4 g, $3, M) and naked action cam + TPU mount (36 g, $199, E). | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L960-963 | must | unit |

| DLA-388 | The cinewhoop must build from 14 parts: FRAME; DUCTS [FRAME]; MOTORS [FRAME]; ESC [FRAME]; MOTOR WIRES [MOTORS, ESC]; FLIGHT CTRL [ESC] (polar arrow); XT60 + CAP [ESC] (polar stripe); O3 CAMERA [FRAME]; AIR UNIT [FC]; RECEIVER [FC]; TOP PLATE [FC, CAM, RX, VTX]; BATTERY [TOP, XT60]; ACTION CAM [TOP]; PROPS [MOTORS, DUCTS, TOP] (polar). | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L966-980 | must | unit |
| DLA-389 | The cinewhoop part texts must teach: 3 mm plate, 147 mm motor to motor; ducts protect and add a little low-speed thrust but cost weight and drag; wide 2004 stators give torque; bidirectional DShot feeds the RPM filter (fixes jello); a soft-mounted gyro. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L967-972 | must | review |
| DLA-390 | The cinewhoop part texts must teach: digital VTX hates electrical noise; ~20° uptilt digital camera with a ribbon to the air unit; the air unit runs hot and needs airflow and antennas; a separate ELRS keeps control if video drops; 4S 1100 is the sweet spot; a caseless action cam records 4K with gyro data; about 1.5 mm duct clearance, props last. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L973-980 | must | review |
| DLA-391 | cinewhoop motors must sit at ±52 mm with duct radii 39.5 / 41.5 mm spanning z −6 to 18, and the bench must tag GOPRO, CAM, VTX, BATT, XT60 and DUCTS. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L981 | must | unit |
| DLA-392 | The cinewhoop must be drawn turned 45° with a 90 × 48 mm plate and arms out to 82 mm, each duct as a far inner wall before the motor and prop and a near outer wall with a dark bumper band and rim after. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L983-985 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-393 | The cinewhoop must draw a green ESC, a blue FC with an arrow (blue LED when powered), and the air unit as a dark box with four metal fins, a green LED and two angled antennas. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L985-986 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-394 | The cinewhoop must draw the O3 camera between two palette plates tilted 20° up with a yellow ribbon to the air unit, a 110 × 56 mm top plate on 28 mm standoffs, the pack at its own size, the action cam on a 14° TPU wedge labelled "4K", and tri-blade props of r 37. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L986-988 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-395 | cinewhoop wiring nodes must be BATTERY, XT60 + CAP, 4-IN-1 ESC "35 A x 4, bidir DShot", FLIGHT CTRL "F7, RPM filter", RECEIVER "ELRS 2.4 GHz", O3 CAMERA "4K sensor, 20 deg", AIR UNIT "digital HD, 1080p", MOTORS x4 and ACTION CAM "own battery, 4K". | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L990-992 | must | e2e |
| DLA-396 | The cinewhoop capacitor size must agree between the part text and the wiring node (the original wiring says "470 uF 35 V" while the part text names no size). | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L990 | must | review |
| DLA-397 | cinewhoop wiring labels must include "M1-M4 + rpm", "VBAT + UART1 (OSD)", "camera cable" and, under the action cam, "no wires: records on its own", with the legend power, data / signal, camera cable, motor phases. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L992-994 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-398 | The cinewhoop over warning must read "{motor name} is a {maxCells}S motor: on {cells}S it spins the ducted props far too fast. Expect hot motors and a desync. On 6S use ~1950 KV.". | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L996 | must | unit |
| DLA-399 | The cinewhoop weak warning must read "Thrust to weight {tw}:1: the action cam and ducts are heavy, so this barely climbs and wobbles in wind. Use bigger motors.", and the two warnings may show together. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L996-997 | must | unit |
| DLA-400 | The cinewhoop OK text must be "Parts match. Plenty of reserve for a heavy action cam." when T:W > 5, else "Parts match. Smooth and gentle: right for cine work.". | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L998 | must | unit |
| DLA-401 | The cinewhoop must add a "Cine (smooth)" altitude-hold mode with hint "Cine mode: gentle sticks, slow yaw, smooth throttle". | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1008 | must | e2e |
| DLA-402 | The cinewhoop race mode must be titled "Gate fly-through" with hint "Thread the gates slowly: you are filming, not racing". | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1031 | must | e2e |
| DLA-403 | In cine and range modes the cinewhoop must scale roll and pitch by 0.45 (about 17° max tilt), yaw by 0.35 (about 70 °/s) and the climb command by 0.6 (1.32 m/s). | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1044-1045 | must | unit |
| DLA-404 | The cinewhoop's angle and acro modes must fly with the limits its own snippets set (angle_limit 30°, soft acro rates) instead of the generic 38° and 540 °/s. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1045-1046; §10 defect 25 | must | unit |
| DLA-405 | The cinewhoop cine snippet must keep roll_rc_rate 50, roll_srate 30 (~150 °/s), yaw_rc_rate 40, rc_smoothing_auto_factor 60, feedforward_smooth_factor 75 and dshot_bidir ON, and must state rates_type = BETAFLIGHT, because those rate values only mean ~150 °/s under BETAFLIGHT rates. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1008-1015; drone-lab-b.md §3 L3300 | must | review |
| DLA-406 | must not copy: the cinewhoop angle snippet's "set p_level = 50" (use angle_p_gain), the acro snippet's rates without a rates_type, and the race snippet's osd_timer_1 / osd_stat_max_spd. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1017-1034 | must | review |
| DLA-407 | The cinewhoop range snippet must keep the 4K/60 with an ND filter for a ~1/120 shutter advice and the soft-sticks settings. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1036-1043 | must | review |
| DLA-408 | The cinewhoop build code must keep align_board_yaw (180 if the arrow points back), DSHOT300, dshot_bidir ON, rpm_filter_harmonics 3, UART1 as MSP DisplayPort with osd_displayport_device = MSP, UART2 = ELRS, and save. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1048-1059 | must | unit |
| DLA-409 | The cinewhoop range "Real-estate fly-through" must have brief "One smooth take: front door, hallway, back door, round the pool, over the roof, land on the drive", a 30 m field, pad (5, 15), ground #dde3cc, altitude hold, FPV tilt 12° and visible race gates. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1061-1062 | must | unit |
| DLA-410 | The real-estate house must span x 10..20, y 9..21 with 2.8 m walls, a 1.0 m front door (y 14.5..15.5, lintel from 2.1 m), a 3 m back sliding door (y 13.5..16.5, lintel from 2.2 m) and a 1.8 m hallway (walls at y 14.1 and 15.9, x 10.2..14). | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1062-1065 | must | unit |
| DLA-411 | The real-estate range must hold the furniture off the y = 15 line, a non-solid inside ceiling, a solid 12 × 12 × 0.25 m roof slab at 2.8 m, a non-solid pitched roof to 4.65 m, the garage house, street, drive, patio and pool decals, the patio table and parasol, two loungers, hedges on y 1 and 29 and five trees. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1065-1068 | must | unit |
| DLA-412 | The real-estate checkpoints must be FRONT DOOR (10, 15, 1.05, r 0.45, ring 0°), HALLWAY (13.8, 15, 1.1, r 0.7, ring 0°), BACK DOOR (20, 15, 1.1, r 0.95, ring 0°), POOL ARC (28.6, 15, 1.6, r 1.3, sphere), OVER THE ROOF (15, 15, 5.9, r 1.4, sphere) and ON THE DRIVE (5, 15, 0.7, r 1.5, sphere). | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1070-1077 | must | unit |
| DLA-413 | The cinewhoop race gates must be visible 1.6 m gates at [5, 9, −90], [12, 4, 0], [25, 5.5, 20], [25, 24, 120] and [6.5, 21.5, 225]. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1079 | must | unit |
| DLA-414 | cinewhoop quiz 1 must ask "What are the ducts really for?" with the right answer "They protect people and props, and add a little thrust at low speed". | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1082 | must | e2e |
| DLA-415 | cinewhoop quiz 2 must ask "Why carry a separate action camera?" with the right answer "It records full-bitrate 4K with gyro data you can stabilise later; the FPV feed is just for flying". | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1083 | must | e2e |
| DLA-416 | cinewhoop quiz 3 must ask "What do you give up for HD digital video?" with the right answer "Some weight, money and a little latency". | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1084 | must | e2e |
| DLA-417 | cinewhoop quiz 4 must ask "Why is a heavy 3-inch less efficient than a 5-inch?" with the right answer "Small props must spin fast to move enough air: less thrust per watt". | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1085 | must | e2e |
| DLA-418 | The cinewhoop overview must present a camera platform, not an acrobat: ducted 3-inch props safe near people, two cameras (digital FPV plus a caseless action cam for stabilised 4K), and the RPM filter keeping jello out. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1087-1089 | must | review |
| DLA-419 | The cinewhoop practice task must ask for the gate fly-through in Cine mode without touching a gate, then again as if filming, and for real a stabilised 30-second indoor take compared with the raw clip. | drone-lab-a.md §9.3 L1089-1090 | must | review |

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |

| DLA-421 | The long-range quad must use mat 430, benchH 150, flyScale 9, flyVB 30000, camZ 0.5, ceiling 70 m, drag 0.045, FPV tilt 20° on its range, CLI name "Betaflight CLI", log name "Betaflight log" and default mode alt. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1095-1098 | must | unit |
| DLA-422 | long-range batteries must be "6S1P Li-ion 4000 mAh" (440 g, $62, #2f6f8f, "6S1P 21700", 70 × 64 × 44), "6S1P Li-ion 5000 mAh (P50B)" (468 g, $78, #7a3f8f, "6S1P P50B", 72 × 64 × 44) and "6S LiPo 1800 mAh" (292 g, $54, #2b2a28, "6S 1800", 104 × 36 × 42). | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1100-1102 | must | unit |
