| DLA-425 | long-range fixed parts must be the 7" true-X frame (162 g, $55, M), 50A 4-in-1 ESC (10 g, $52, E), F7 FC + baro (8 g, $45, E), analog camera (7 g, $25, E), 5.8 GHz 1 W VTX + stubby (10 g, $38, E), ELRS 900 MHz diversity RX + T-antenna (4 g, $32, E), M10 GPS + QMC5883 compass (12 g, $24, E), XT60 + cap (12 g, $4, E), top plate + standoffs + GPS mast (30 g, $12, M) and strap + grip pad (6 g, $4, M). | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1107-1110 | must | unit |
| DLA-426 | The long-range default design must show 925 g, 1333 g per motor, 5.8:1, hover 42 %, 120 A, 36.9 min hover, 15.5 min aggressive and $394 + $81 = $475. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1110-1111 | must | unit |
| DLA-427 | The long-range quad must build from 13 parts: FRAME; MOTORS [FRAME]; ESC [FRAME]; MOTOR WIRES [MOTORS, ESC]; FLIGHT CTRL [ESC] (polar); XT60 + CAP [ESC] (polar); CAMERA [FRAME]; RECEIVER [FC]; VTX [FC]; TOP PLATE [FC, CAM, RX, VTX]; GPS [TOP] (polar "arrow forward" / "arrow backward"); BATTERY [TOP, XT60]; PROPS [MOTORS, TOP] (polar). | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1113-1126 | must | unit |
| DLA-428 | The long-range part texts must teach: 6 mm arms, 315 mm motor to motor, longer arms flex more; low KV for big props, grams per watt buys flight time; the ESC runs cool at 30-40 % cruise; long leads tight and sleeved; GPS Rescue firmware with a barometer; a 1000 µF cap; only 15-20° camera uptilt for flat cruising. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1114-1120 | must | review |
| DLA-429 | The long-range part texts must teach: ELRS 900 MHz goes further and through trees (watch link quality); a 1 W VTX fades before control, hence GPS Rescue; a printed mast lifts the GPS from carbon and the VTX; wait for 8+ satellites with the GPS arrow to the nose; 21700 Li-ion gives more energy per gram but limited burst current; bi-blades are most efficient, props last. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1121-1126 | must | review |
| DLA-430 | long-range motors must sit at ±111.4 mm (315 mm wheelbase) and the bench must tag GPS, FC, RX, VTX, XT60, MOTORS, BATT and CAM. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1127 | must | unit |
| DLA-431 | The long-range quad must draw a 132 × 52 mm plate with 132 mm arms, ESC, FC and XT60 as on the 5-inch but bigger, a camera lens tilted 18° up, a standing T-antenna behind the stack and a 1 W VTX with an angled stubby. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1129-1130 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-432 | The long-range quad must draw a 124 × 44 mm top plate with a palette GPS mast leaning back 22°, a white GPS puck with an ink arrow that flips with orientation and a green LED when powered. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1131-1132 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-433 | The long-range quad must draw the pack at its own dims with two cell-line marks on Li-ion packs, motor bells of r 17 and bi- or tri-blades at the selected radius (89 or 95 mm). | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1132-1133 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-434 | long-range wiring nodes must be BATTERY, XT60 + CAP "1000 uF 35 V", 4-IN-1 ESC "50 A x 4, BLHeli_32", FLIGHT CTRL "F7 + baro, Betaflight", RECEIVER "ELRS 900 MHz", GPS + COMPASS "M10 + QMC5883", CAMERA "analog, 18 deg", VTX "5.8 GHz, 1 W" and MOTORS x4. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1135-1137 | must | e2e |
| DLA-435 | long-range wiring labels must be "UART2 + 5V", "UART3", "I2C mag" (blue dashed), "video in" and "video + SmartAudio", with the legend power, data / UART, I2C, video, motor phases. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1137-1138 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-436 | The long-range over warning must read "{kv} KV on 6S spins a 7" prop far too fast: hot motors and a desync, a long way from home. Use 1100-1300 KV on 6S.". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1140 | must | unit |
| DLA-437 | The long-range weak warning must read "Thrust to weight {tw}:1 is too low even for cruising: no margin to climb over trees or fight wind.". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1141 | must | unit |
| DLA-438 | A Li-ion long-range build with max current above 45 A must warn "Full throttle pulls ~{round(maxA)} A, more than one 21700 cell can give (~45 A): the voltage will sag hard on punch-outs. Fine if you cruise.". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1142 | must | unit |
| DLA-439 | A LiPo long-range build must note "LiPo: lots of punch, but only {flyMin.toFixed(0)} min of cruise. Li-ion more than doubles it.", and the long-range warnings may stack. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1140, L1143 | must | unit |
| DLA-440 | The long-range OK text "Parts match. A calm cruiser: about {flyMin} min of flight." must be reachable by at least one design, or replaced by a message that can actually appear (in the original every design gets the Li-ion or LiPo note). | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1144-1145; §10 defect 26 | must | unit |
| DLA-441 | The long-range build code must keep resource, align_board_yaw = 0 (180 if the FC arrow points back), align_mag = DEFAULT (puck arrow to the nose), feature GPS, serial 2 2 57600 57600 0 115200 (UART3 = GPS), gps_provider = UBLOX, gps_ublox_use_galileo = ON, failsafe_procedure = GPS-RESCUE and save. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1147-1159 | must | unit |
| DLA-442 | The long-range quad must fly alt, angle, acro and race, then "GPS rescue (failsafe demo)" (altitude hold, hint "Hands off: it flies out, loses link, then GPS Rescue brings it home"), then its range. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1160-1161 | must | e2e |
| DLA-443 | The rescue code must keep failsafe_procedure = GPS-RESCUE, gps_rescue_min_sats = 8, gps_rescue_return_alt = 45 (metres), gps_rescue_ground_speed = 750 (cm/s), gps_rescue_descent_dist = 20 and gps_rescue_sanity_checks = RESCUE_SANITY_FS_ONLY, with the advice to test close in first. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1162-1172 | must | unit |
| DLA-444 | In rescue mode, armed and not crashed, the autopilot must take the sticks (the pilot loses throttle and attitude input). | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1174-1175 | must | e2e |
| DLA-445 | Rescue started at the pad (within 2.5 m, z < 0.5) must begin with the "out" leg; started anywhere else it must climb at once with "Failsafe! GPS Rescue: climbing to 45 m, over the ridges" (more than 30 m from home) or "Failsafe! GPS Rescue: climbing to 7 m", logging "failsafe -> GPS RESCUE". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1176-1178 | must | e2e |
| DLA-446 | The rescue climb command must be clamp((target − z) · 0.6, ±1). | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1179 | must | unit |
| DLA-447 | The rescue navigation must command speed min(vmax, 0.7·dist) toward the target, yaw = clamp(−1.6 · wrap(bearing − heading), ±1) beyond 2.5 m, and pitch and roll = clamp(0.2 · body-frame velocity error, ±0.7), using a heading estimate that is 180° off when the GPS puck is reversed. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1180-1182 | must | unit |
| DLA-448 | The out leg must climb to 4.5 m over home, hold 5 m and fly to pad + (11, 4) at up to 5 m/s, then within 1.6 m show "Link lost! Failsafe: GPS Rescue takes over" (error), log "RX LOSS -> failsafe -> GPS RESCUE" and start the climb. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1183-1184 | must | e2e |
| DLA-449 | The return altitude must be chosen once: 45 m when more than 30 m from home, else 7 m. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1185 | must | unit |
| DLA-450 | The climb phase must hold position and climb to the return altitude + 0.3 m, then log "rescue: climb done, heading home". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1186 | must | e2e |
| DLA-451 | The home phase must hold altitude and fly home at up to 12 m/s (above 10 m) or 6 m/s, and log "rescue: over home, descending" within 1 m of home. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1187-1189 | must | e2e |
| DLA-452 | With the GPS puck reversed and more than 14 m from home, rescue must warn once "Compass arrow backwards: it flies AWAY from home. Fix align_mag" (buzz) and log "rescue: heading error, distance growing". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1187-1188 | must | e2e |
| DLA-453 | The descend phase must hold over home at up to 1.2 m/s with climb command −1 above 6 m, −0.5 above 1.5 m, else −0.22, and on touchdown (z ≤ 0.01, t > 1 s) disarm with "Landed at home and disarmed" (ding) and log "rescue: landed, disarmed". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1190-1191; §4.7 L447 | must | e2e |
| DLA-454 | The rescue demo from the pad must take about 17.5 s, and with a reversed puck the craft must fly away at about 12 m/s until it leaves the field. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1192-1193; Appendix B L1438-1439 | must | unit |
| DLA-455 | Rescue started 94 m out must climb to 45 m at about 2.1 m/s and return at about 9 m/s. | drone-lab-a.md Appendix B L1440 | must | unit |
| DLA-456 | During rescue the iso view must draw a green dashed line from the craft's ground point to home, and the stats must add "Home" {dist} m and "Rescue" OUT / CLIMB / HOME / DESCEND / DONE. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1193-1194; §1.3 L111 | must | e2e |
| DLA-457 | The long-range range "Mountain valley" must have brief "Follow the river, over the saddle, touch the tower, come home", a 300 m field, pad (60, 60), ground #d9e2c4, visible race gates, FPV tilt 20° and altitude hold. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1196-1197 | must | unit |
| DLA-458 | The mountain valley must hold the valley road, the river as four 9 m strips to a lake disc (272, 150) r 20, the farmhouse, the barn, about 19 trees including the forest, a ridge of seven cones at y 205 with a low saddle hump at x 170, two back hills, and a 40 m radio tower with a red mast to 48 m and a hut. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1197-1202 | must | unit |
| DLA-459 | The mountain-valley checkpoints must be RIVER (150, 115, 6, r 6, ring 15°), LAKE (262, 146, 8, r 6, sphere), SADDLE (170, 205, 22, r 6, ring 90°), TOWER TOP (200, 265, 53, r 6, sphere) and HOME (60, 60, 2, r 4, sphere). | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1204-1210 | must | unit |
| DLA-460 | The long-range race gates must be the default five shifted by (+45, +45): [63.5, 60, 0], [66.5, 63.5, 70], [63, 66.5, 165], [58, 65, 215], [57, 60.5, −60], visible, 1.6 m. | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1212-1213 | must | unit |
| DLA-461 | long-range quiz 1 must ask "Why fly long range on a Li-ion pack instead of a LiPo?" with the right answer "More energy per gram, so longer flights, at the cost of burst current". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1216 | must | e2e |
| DLA-462 | long-range quiz 2 must ask "What actually triggers GPS Rescue?" with the right answer "A failsafe: the flight controller stops hearing your radio". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1217 | must | e2e |
| DLA-463 | long-range quiz 3 must ask "Why wait for 8 or more satellites before take-off?" with the right answer "So the home point is accurate: rescue flies back to wherever home was set". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1218 | must | e2e |
| DLA-464 | long-range quiz 4 must ask "The compass arrow points backwards. What does GPS Rescue do?" with the right answer "Flies the wrong way or circles (a toilet bowl)". | drone-lab-a.md §9.4 L1219 | must | e2e |

## DLA · Original defects 1-31 (must not copy) and the §11 improvements

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DLA-467 | must not copy: re-orienting a placed part without restarting the animation loop (defect 1); a re-oriented part must animate back to fitted and Parts n/N must update at once. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1232; §4.2 L331-335 | must | e2e |
| DLA-468 | must not copy: a design change in the flight view while disarmed nulling the flight (defect 2); the change must start a fresh flight on the pad or be refused with a message, never leave the bench drawn under the flight UI with Space dead. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1233-1234; §11 L1288 | must | e2e |
| DLA-469 | must not copy: switching from altitude hold to a manual-throttle mode mid-air zeroing the throttle (defect 3); the manual throttle must start at the current effective throttle so the craft keeps flying. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1235; §7.1 L507-508 | must | e2e |
| DLA-470 | must not copy: a new flight message inheriting the old message's timer (defect 4); every message must stay up for its full time from when it appears. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1236 | must | unit |
| DLA-471 | must not copy: the invisible 5-inch race gates and a course that crosses the gap walls (defect 5); the 5-inch race gates must be visible solids on a course that can be lapped cleanly, since the practice task asks for five laps. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1237-1238; §9.1 L836-838; §11 L1284 | must | e2e |
| DLA-472 | must not copy: the unreachable TOWER TOP checkpoint (defect 6: 12 m soft ceiling, 10.47 m peak, nearest legal approach 2.38 m); a scripted flight must be able to complete all five bando checkpoints. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1239-1241; Appendix B L1430 | must | e2e |
| DLA-473 | must not copy: a hovering craft that never cuts out (defect 7, 780 mAh of 1100 still at 3.67 V/cell after 600 s); an exhausted pack must reach the cutoff at hover and cut the motors. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1242; Appendix B L1433; §11 L1277-1278 | must | unit |
| DLA-474 | must not copy: thrust that ignores pack voltage (defect 7); thrust must scale with (V/Vnom)², so a sagging or nearly empty pack loses punch. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1242; §11 L1278 | must | unit |
| DLA-475 | must not copy: the same 0.002 V per amp per cell sag for every pack (defect 7); sag must come from per-cell internal resistance scaled by capacity, so a 1S whoop pack sags visibly under load. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1242-1243; §11 L1277-1278 | must | unit |
| DLA-476 | must not copy: Li-ion and HV packs using the LiPo curve and LiPo messages (defect 7); LiPo (4.2 V full), LiHV (4.35 V full) and Li-ion (4.2 V to 2.8 V) must each use their own curve and their own empty message. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1243-1244; §11 L1277 | must | unit |
| DLA-477 | must not copy: imperceptible wind with no indicator (defect 8: a hovering 5-inch moves 0.2 m in 5 s); wind must act as airspeed (drag on v − wind), gust by several m/s and be shown by a visible indicator. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1245; §7.3 L567; §11 L1279 | must | e2e |
| DLA-478 | must not copy: a mild desync and heat that never cools (defect 9: a 13° lean at 92 % thrust); a desync must lose a motor (asymmetric thrust, yaw spin) and motor heat must cool when the throttle drops. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1246; §11 L1280 | must | unit |
| DLA-479 | must not copy: the inverted OSD horizon pitch (defect 10); the horizon bar must move up when the nose drops, as the real horizon does. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1247; §7.8 L687-689; §11 L1281 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-480 | must not copy: "Build it for me" passing every test while reversed parts stay reversed (defect 11); it must either fit every part the right way round or leave the related tests failed, so a reversed cap still pops. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1248; §4.3 L353 | must | e2e |
| DLA-481 | must not copy: the motor check plugging in without the reversed-cap check (defect 12); any automatic plug-in must run the same checks as "Plug / unplug battery". | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1249; §4.4 L381 | must | e2e |
| DLA-482 | must not copy: wrong collision labels (defect 13: every box "Hit an obstacle", ridges and the saddle "Hit a tree"); each solid must carry its own kind so the crash names what was actually hit. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1250; §7.6 L638-641 | must | e2e |
| DLA-483 | must not copy: no cutaway in the iso view (defect 14); the craft must stay visible inside the bando, the house and under roofs. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1251; §7.7 L666-667; §11 L1282-1283 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-484 | must not copy: a collision radius that does not match the drawn size (defect 15: 0.14 m against a 5-inch drawn about 1 m across); what is drawn at flight scale and what collides must agree. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1252 | must | unit |
| DLA-485 | must not copy: a per-frame camera follow factor (defect 16); the follow must be time-based so it feels the same at 30, 60 and 120 Hz. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1253; §7.7 L660 | must | unit |
| DLA-486 | must not copy: `?fly=<unknown>` throwing (defect 17); every URL parameter must be validated and an unknown or malformed value ignored without an error. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1254; §1.7 L168-169; §11 L1289 | must | e2e |
| DLA-487 | must not copy: the keyboard throttle dying after the touch throttle was used in a manual mode (defect 18); releasing the touch stick must hand throttle back to the keys. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1255 | must | e2e |
| DLA-488 | must not copy: re-arming after a GPS-rescue landing leaving the autopilot on with no throttle (defect 19); a finished rescue must release the sticks to the pilot. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1256 | must | e2e |
| DLA-489 | must not copy: one best time shared by race laps and range runs (defect 20); best times must be kept per course and per craft. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1257; §11 L1285 | must | unit |
| DLA-490 | must not copy: lap and crash counts that are never shown (defect 20); the flight view must show both. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1257; §11 L1285 | must | e2e |
| DLA-491 | must not copy: the range clock starting at checkpoint 1 (defect 21); the run clock must start at take-off. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1258; §7.6 L645 | must | e2e |
| DLA-492 | must not copy: raw float output such as "battery plugged: 25.200000000000003 V" (defect 22); every displayed number must be rounded for display. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1259 | must | unit |
| DLA-493 | must not copy: the Effects preference read but never saved (defect 23); the Lofi and Effects choices must persist in localStorage. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1260; §8 L713 | must | e2e |
| DLA-494 | must not copy: three different camera tilts per craft (defect 24: part text ~30/20/20/15-20°, wiring 30/20/20/18°, FPV 25/15/12/20°); one tilt value per craft must drive the part text, wiring label, drawing and FPV view. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1261; §11 L1276 | must | unit |
| DLA-495 | must not copy: CLI snippets that disagree with the sim (defect 25); the angle limit, rates and deadband in a craft's snippets must be the values its flight model uses. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1262; §11 L1276 | must | unit |
| DLA-496 | must not copy: the label "Freestyle time" on the whoop, cinewhoop and long-range quad (defect 26); the sixth tile must be labelled per craft (Freestyle time, Indoor time, Cine time, Cruise time). | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1263; §11 L1274-1275 | must | e2e |
| DLA-497 | must not copy: the 5-inch with no noun, so its card reads "Meet the drone" (defect 26); every craft must name what it is. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1263 | must | e2e |
| DLA-498 | must not copy: the motor check always finding M3 reversed (defect 27); the reversed motor must be chosen at random per wiring job or derived from the wire order. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1264; §11 L1287 | must | unit |
| DLA-499 | must not copy: unreachable fault paths (defect 27: the "motor direction never checked" flip and the uncalibrated-ACC drift); every fault kept in the model must be reachable from the UI, or removed. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1264; §11 L1287; §7.3 L540 | must | e2e |
| DLA-500 | must not copy: craft shadow points defined but never drawn (defect 28); every craft must cast its shadow on the bench and in the field, following what is built. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1265; §11 L1283 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-501 | must not copy: the selected part's pill drawn at a fixed spot above the craft (defect 29); the pill must sit at the selected part and move with it. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1266; §4.6 L425 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-502 | must not copy: calculator tiles and phone bench stats that wrap (defect 30: "514" / "g" on two lines); each value must stay on one line at desktop and phone widths. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1267 | must | screenshot |
| DLA-503 | must not copy: max current never compared with the ESC or pack rating (defect 31: 282 A on a 4 × 45 A ESC); the calculator must warn when max current exceeds the ESC's total continuous rating or what the pack can deliver. | drone-lab-a.md §10 L1268; §11 L1275 | must | unit |
| DLA-504 | The iso flight view should show a home arrow and the distance home, as the OSD does. | drone-lab-a.md §11 L1281 | should | e2e |
| DLA-505 | The race should keep a gate list with per-gate split times. | drone-lab-a.md §11 L1284-1285 | should | e2e |
| DLA-506 | A design change made during a flight should be offered as "Apply to next flight" instead of being lost or breaking the flight. | drone-lab-a.md §11 L1288 | should | e2e |
| DLA-507 | The iso flight view should let the learner see the craft through roofs and floors with see-through, while the world itself stays a host-drawn view rather than a Colorfigures figure. | drone-lab-a.md §11 L1282-1283; colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1033 | should | screenshot |

| DLB-003 | An autopilot must fly a multirotor through stick commands, turning a wanted acceleration a into a self-level stick of atan(a/g) / 38°. | drone-lab-b.md §1 L61-66 | must | unit |
| DLB-004 | While an autopilot flies it must overwrite yaw, pitch and roll on every physics step, and the keys must not write throttle. | drone-lab-b.md §1 L83-84 | must | unit |

| DLB-008 | A collision crash must name what was hit ("Hit a tree", "Hit a roof", "Hit an obstacle" or "Hit {name}"). | drone-lab-b.md §1 L92-93 | must | e2e |
| DLB-009 | The bench stats strip's "Flight time" must use the same flight-time figure as the craft's own tiles (the wing's strip says ~24.4 min against a 31 min tile). | drone-lab-b.md §1 L58-59; §2 L155; Fig 09 L1881 | must | e2e |
| DLB-010 | Every craft's Fly menu must list its own modes (default first), "Range: {title}", "FPV view on / off (V)", "Wind on / off" and "Back to the bench". | drone-lab-b.md §2 L130-143 | must | e2e |
| DLB-011 | must not copy: the roster placeholders ("Fig. NN · soon", "FPV fixed wing" / "INAV, flaps"); no placeholder text may ever render. | drone-lab-b.md §2 L144-145; SPEC.md L123 | must | e2e |
| DLB-012 | The bench flow of every Fig 05-13 craft must enforce the same rules as the engine: prerequisites, props last after the smoke and motor checks, nothing fitted while plugged. | drone-lab-b.md §1 L114-126 | must | e2e |

| DLB-057 | In racer FPV the camera must tilt up 38° with prop blurs at the top corners and the OSD arrow must point at the next gate ("GATE n/8"). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L426-430 | must | e2e |

| DLB-059 | The racer build code must keep DSHOT600, dshot_bidir = ON, align_board_yaw = 0, the RACEBAND vtxtable (5658 5695 5732 5769 5806 5843 5880 5917), vtx_band 1, vtx_channel 4, vtx_power 1 (25 mW), vtx_pit_mode_freq 5584, led 0 0,0::C:6 and save. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L439-457 | must | unit |
| DLB-060 | must not copy: the incomplete vtxtable (no "vtxtable channels" and no power-level lines); the snippet must be a loadable table. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L475-476 | must | review |
| DLB-061 | must not copy: "set osd_timer_1 = ON" and "set osd_stat_max_spd = ON" in the race, range and build panels; the timer is the packed value osd_tim1 with its element position osd_tim_1_pos (not "osd_timer_1_pos" as the research note says), and post-flight stats are chosen with osd_stat_bitmask. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L326-327, L412-413, L475-476; §3 L3314 | must | review |
| DLB-062 | must not copy: "set ledstrip_race_color = 6"; Betaflight takes a colour name here (index 6 is GREEN). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L455; §3 L3297-3320 | must | review |
| DLB-063 | The racer practice task must ask for 3 clean angle-mode laps in a row, then acro, noting the best lap, then 10 % less max rate and 10 more points of expo for 5 more laps, comparing the spread. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L459-461 | must | review |
| DLB-064 | racer quiz 1 must ask "Why does a race VTX boot in pit mode?" with the right answer "So it never blasts other pilots' video while you plug in". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L465 | must | e2e |
| DLB-065 | racer quiz 2 must ask "What does adding expo do to your rates?" with the right answer "Makes small stick moves gentler without lowering the max rate". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L466 | must | e2e |
| DLB-066 | racer quiz 3 must ask "Why a stretched-X frame for racing?" with the right answer "The rear props stay out of the front props' dirty air at speed". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L467 | must | e2e |
| DLB-067 | racer quiz 4 must ask "Your 4S race quad feels sluggish with 1950 KV motors. Why?" with the right answer "1950 KV is a 6S motor: on 4S the props spin too slowly". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L468 | must | e2e |

| DLB-145 | The delivery drone must draw 47 mm motor bells on 52 mm mounts, #2f4f7a ESCs halfway out each arm, the PDB with a capacitor stripe showing its orientation and a yellow XT90. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L915-917 | must | screenshot |
| DLB-146 | The delivery drone must draw the Pixhawk 6C (82 × 44 × 14 mm) on a black damping plate with a red stripe, "PIXHAWK", a red nose arrow and green LED, and the nose depth camera with two blue lenses and a purple IR projector. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L918-919 | must | screenshot |
| DLB-147 | The delivery drone must draw the top plate on 40 mm standoffs with the battery (176 mm, or 200 mm above 12000 mAh), the green Pi 5 with two USB blocks and a fan, the LTE board with two 65 mm antennas, a 129 mm GPS mast with a 72 mm puck, the telemetry antenna and the RC receiver's two antennas. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L920-923 | must | screenshot |
| DLB-148 | The delivery bench must tag FC, COMP, GPS, DEPTH, CARGO, MOTORS, BATT and XT60, and its motors must follow ArduPilot quad X (M1 front-right CCW, M2 rear-left CCW, M3 front-left CW, M4 rear-right CW). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L924-927 | must | unit |
| DLB-149 | The delivery drone must build from 16 parts in this forced order: FRAME, MOTORS, ESC, WIRES, XT60 (PDB + CAP, stripe), FC (arrow), RX, TEL, DEPTH, TOP, COMP, LTE, GPS, CARGO (layer −5, dropping below the frame when exploded), BATT, PROPS (props in / swapped). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L929-948, L975-976 | must | unit |
| DLB-150 | The delivery part texts must teach: folding arm latches must click home or it drops; low-KV motors swinging big props are efficient (about 2.5 kg each flat out on 6S); ESCs on the arms cooled by prop wash; long leads add inductance and spikes; the PDB makes 5 V and 12 V and its 1000 µF cap soaks spikes; Pixhawk 6C with three IMUs, baro and EKF3 on damping balls. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L950-959 | must | review |
| DLB-151 | The delivery part texts must teach: a backup radio so a human can take over; SiK 433 MHz MAVLink and the GCS failsafe; depth camera for obstacle avoidance and SLAM on approach; the Pi 5 runs MAVSDK or ROS 2 while real-time control stays on the Pixhawk; LTE beyond line of sight needs permission; the winch on SERVOn_FUNCTION = 88 keeps props away from people. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L960-973 | must | review |
| DLB-152 | The delivery plug-in log must print a rounded voltage ("battery plugged: 25.2 V"). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L980-981, L1178 | must | unit |
| DLB-153 | delivery wiring blocks must be WINCH + BAY "servo PWM from AUX1", LTE MODEM "4G, USB to the Pi", RASPBERRY PI 5 "companion, MAVSDK", DEPTH CAMERA "USB3, SLAM", RC RECEIVER "backup, CRSF", TELEMETRY "SiK 433 MHz", PIXHAWK 6C "ArduCopter, 3 IMUs", GPS + COMPASS "M10, UART + I2C", BATTERY, PDB + CAP "XT90, 5 V + 12 V BEC", ESCs x4 "40 A, DShot" and MOTORS x4, at the listed positions. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L983-1000 | must | e2e |
| DLB-154 | delivery wiring lines must carry "22.2V", "3-phase x4", "DShot", "5 V", "5 V 5 A", "MAVLink", "UART + I2C", "CRSF", "USB3", "USB" and "winch PWM", with the legend power, data / MAVLink / USB, motors + winch. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1002-1004 | must | screenshot |
| DLB-155 | The delivery drone must fly "Deliver a parcel (auto)" (default; hint "Autopilot: take off, fly to the drop zone, winch the parcel down"), "Loiter (GPS hold)" ("Let go of the arrows: the GPS holds position") and "Return to launch" ("Climbs to 12 m (above the roofs), flies home, lands"). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1006-1038 | must | e2e |
| DLB-156 | must not copy: the MAVSDK snippet's placeholders (DROP_LAT, DROP_LON, ALT_AMSL) and the winch left as a comment; the snippet must be runnable code with real values and a real winch step (MAV_CMD_DO_WINCH, or MAVSDK's separate Winch plugin), keeping the serial connect at 921600, set_takeoff_altitude(12), arm, takeoff, goto_location and return_to_launch. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1010-1026, L1176-1177 | must | review |
| DLB-157 | The delivery Loiter params must keep LOIT_SPEED 500, LOIT_ACC_MAX 250 (gentle with a parcel on a line), PILOT_SPEED_UP 150 and EK3_SRC1_POSXY 3 (GPS). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1028-1036 | must | unit |
| DLB-158 | The delivery RTL params must keep RTL_ALT 1200, RTL_SPEED 300, FS_GCS_ENABLE 1 and BATT_LOW_VOLT 21.0 (3.5 V per cell), and must use BATT_FS_LOW_ACT 2 in place of the pre-3.6 FS_BATT_ENABLE 2. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1040-1047, L1175 | must | review |
| DLB-159 | The delivery autopilot must use depot pad (24, 45), drop zones (38, 65), (66, 25), (78, 66), cruise 12 m (clears 7 m roofs and 9 m poles), hover 3.5 m and a 1000 g payload. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1052-1054 | must | unit |
| DLB-160 | must not copy: the auto mission flying only to drop zone 1; the delivery mission must be able to serve all three drop zones. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1052-1053, L1172; §4 L3346-3357 | must | e2e |
| DLB-161 | The delivery autopilot must steer at speed min(vmax, 0.7 · distance) with acceleration 1.4 × velocity error converted by k = 1/(9.81 · tan 38°) and clamped to ±0.5 (about 19° tilt), yaw clamp(−1.2 · heading error, ±0.5) beyond 2.5 m, and throttle clamp(0.9 · (tz − z) − 0.25 · vz, ±1). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1058-1061 | must | unit |
| DLB-162 | The delivery mission must run the phases takeoff (to 12 m, exit above 11.6 m), to drop zone ("Flying to the drop zone", up to 5 m/s), descend (to 3.5 m), lowering ("Holding position, lowering the parcel", line out at 0.9 m/s), release, winch up (1.2 m/s), climb ("Winch stowed, climbing"), flying home ("Returning to launch"), landing ("Landing") and delivered, logging "mission: {phase}" on each. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1055-1073 | must | e2e |
| DLB-163 | The release phase must say "Parcel on the ground: releasing" with the snap sound, then drop the parcel 0.6 s later: AUW −1000 g, hover throttle recomputed, log "payload released: AUW -1000 g, hover throttle drops". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1069 | must | e2e |
| DLB-164 | Landing must hold over the pad at throttle −0.7 above 1.2 m and −0.35 below, and finish on the ground (abs(vz) < 0.3 m/s for 1 s) with a disarm, ding and "Delivered! Landed back on the pad". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1073-1074 | must | e2e |
| DLB-165 | Arming in Deliver after a drop must reload the parcel with "New parcel loaded: off we go" and recompute the calculator. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1076-1077 | must | e2e |

| DLB-168 | must not copy: Loiter to RTL in the air doing nothing while locking the pilot out until R; switching to RTL mid-flight must fly home. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1088-1089, L1171 | must | e2e |
| DLB-169 | The delivery mission must reproduce the original timing within tolerance: drop-zone leg 6.1 s, descend 14.0 s, lowering 18.7 s, releasing 22.7 s, released 23.3 s (AUW 5177 → 4177 g, hover 0.716 → 0.643), winch up 24.1 s, climbing 27.2 s, returning 31.6 s, landing 39.5 s, delivered 48.4 s, about 337 mAh. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1081-1085 | must | unit |
| DLB-170 | At drop zone 1 the field must draw a cream disc (r 0.9 m) with a red ring (r 0.56 m) and a red X; the winch line must hang from the belly by its length with the parcel until release, then a metal hook; the dropped parcel must stay on the lawn. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1093-1095 | must | screenshot |
| DLB-171 | Delivery flight stats must add "Mission" {phase} and "Parcel" "aboard" / "delivered". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1095-1096 | must | e2e |

| DLB-173 | The suburb must hold two crossing 6 m streets with centre lines, the depot (10 × 16 × 6 m) with a loading-bay door and two crates, eight houses with 3 m roofs, lawns, 1.2 m fences, drop-zone discs labelled 1-3, six 9 m power poles with non-solid wires at 8.6 m, a school, a playground, a hexagonal corner shop and twelve trees. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1101-1106 | must | unit |

| DLB-176 | The delivery drone must flip at about 0.8 s with props swapped, and 2814 700KV on 6S must desync after about 34 s. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1126-1129 | must | e2e |
| DLB-177 | The arm-latch failure the delivery lesson teaches should be simulated, not only described (the GCS-link failsafe is covered by the ArduPilot failsafe requirement). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1130; §4 L3350-3352 | should | e2e |
| DLB-178 | Delivery FPV must be gimbal-level and point 15° down on the range. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1134 | must | e2e |
| DLB-179 | "Meet the delivery drone" must explain two computers in one airframe (the Pixhawk real-time, the Pi mission logic, depth camera and 4G over MAVLink), that it hovers and winches instead of landing at the door, and to watch the hover throttle drop at release. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1136-1141 | must | review |
| DLB-180 | The delivery build params must keep FRAME_CLASS 1, FRAME_TYPE 1, AHRS_ORIENTATION 0 (4 if the arrow points back), SERIAL2_PROTOCOL 2, SERIAL2_BAUD 921, SERVO9_FUNCTION 88, WINCH_TYPE 1, MOT_PWM_TYPE 6 and FS_GCS_ENABLE 1. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1143-1156; §3 L3297-3320 | must | unit |
| DLB-181 | The delivery practice task must ask for a three-waypoint SITL mission with DO_WINCH at the drop point, flown from a MAVSDK script, cutting telemetry mid-flight to check the GCS failsafe and noting how far it flew before turning back. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1158-1160 | must | review |
| DLB-182 | delivery quiz 1 must ask "Why lower the parcel on a winch instead of landing at the door?" with the right answer "The props stay high above people, pets and fences". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1164 | must | e2e |
| DLB-183 | delivery quiz 2 must ask "Which board does the real-time stabilisation?" with the right answer "The Pixhawk". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1165 | must | e2e |
| DLB-184 | delivery quiz 3 must ask "Why is the GPS up on a mast?" with the right answer "To keep the compass away from the magnetic field of the power wiring". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1166 | must | e2e |
| DLB-185 | delivery quiz 4 must ask "The parcel is released. What happens to the hover throttle?" with the right answer "It drops: the drone is 1 kg lighter". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 07 L1167 | must | e2e |

| DLB-314 | The wing controls card must read W S "throttle (throttle up to hand-launch)", arrows "pitch + roll (the elevons)", A D "nothing: no rudder, it turns by banking", Space "arm, then W to throw it", R "reset to the pad", plus "or drag the two sticks on the figure". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 09 L1838-1840 | must | e2e |

| DLB-316 | "Meet the wing" must explain the tailless wing with two elevons and sweep for stability, one motor with about half the weight in thrust and 20 to 40 minutes of flight, lift growing with the square of airspeed and the stall, balancing at the CG mark, checking elevon direction and throwing it level into the wind. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 09 L1842-1847 | must | review |
| DLB-317 | The wing lesson must describe the stall as the wing passing its critical angle of attack, which happens when airspeed drops too low, matching quiz 2. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 09 L1846, L1871 | must | review |

| DLB-319 | The wing practice task must ask to balance the wing on two fingertips at the CG marks, check the elevons with the sticks and by tilting it in angle mode (they should fight the tilt), make three angle-mode hand launches, fly a figure-eight, then switch to RTH. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 09 L1864-1866 | must | review |
| DLB-320 | Wing quiz 1 must ask "What do elevons do?" with the right answer "Act as elevator and ailerons at once". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 09 L1870 | must | e2e |
| DLB-321 | Wing quiz 2 must ask "When does a wing stall?" with the right answer "When the airspeed drops too low (angle of attack too high)". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 09 L1871 | must | e2e |
| DLB-322 | Wing quiz 3 must ask "The wing is tail-heavy at the CG check. In the air it will..." with the right answer "pitch up, stall and be hard to recover". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 09 L1872 | must | e2e |
| DLB-323 | Wing quiz 4 must ask "Why check elevon direction on the bench before the first launch?" with the right answer "A reversed servo makes the stabiliser push the wrong way: it dives straight in". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 09 L1873 | must | e2e |

| DLB-394 | The VTOL build params must keep Q_ENABLE 1 (reboot), Q_FRAME_CLASS 7, SERVO1_FUNCTION 75 (TiltMotorFrontLeft), SERVO2_FUNCTION 76, SERVO1_REVERSED 0 (check with the tilt test before flying), ARSPD_TYPE 1 (MS4525 on I2C), ARSPD_USE 1 and AHRS_ORIENTATION 0 (4 = yaw 180). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 10 L2235-2247; §3 L3297-3320 | must | review |

| DLB-396 | VTOL quiz 1 must ask "Why does a VTOL fly so much longer than a quad on the same battery?" with the right answer "In cruise the wing carries the weight, the motors only fight drag". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 10 L2256 | must | e2e |
| DLB-397 | VTOL quiz 2 must ask "The tilt servos are reversed. When does it crash?" with the right answer "At the start of the transition, when the motors swing back". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 10 L2257 | must | e2e |
| DLB-398 | VTOL quiz 3 must ask "Why use an airspeed sensor instead of GPS speed?" with the right answer "The wing only cares about air speed: with a tailwind GPS reads fast while the wing stalls". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 10 L2258 | must | e2e |
| DLB-399 | VTOL quiz 4 must ask "What does Q_ASSIST_SPEED do?" with the right answer "Spins the lift motors up when airspeed drops below it, so a slow wing does not stall". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 10 L2259 | must | e2e |

| DLB-470 | The tricopter part texts must teach: the capacitor stripe to minus; the camera only 15-20° up; the TRI mixer with three motor outputs and one servo output; RX antennas away from carbon; never power a VTX without its antenna; the centre of thrust sits further back than you think; 4S is plenty and the CG is where it hovers level; the tail's leftover torque is trimmed with a few degrees of servo tilt. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2693-2701 | must | review |
| DLB-471 | "Servo test" must refuse in flight ("Land first"), need TAILSERVO and FC ("Mount the tail servo and the flight controller first") but not the battery, log "$ servo tab: sweep servo 5 (tail)  set tri_unarmed_servo = ON", and sweep the tail 28° · sin(4t) (mirrored when reversed) for 3.2 s. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2707-2711 | must | e2e |
| DLB-472 | The servo test must end with log "yaw right -> tail tilts left, servo centred at 1500 us" and "Yaw right tilts the tail motor left: that pushes the tail left and swings the nose right. Correct" (ding), or for a reversed servo log "yaw right -> tail tilted the WRONG way" and "Yaw right tilted the tail the wrong way: the servo is reversed. Re-mount it (Add next part, TAIL SERVO: servo normal) or set smix reverse" (buzz). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2712-2715 | must | e2e |
| DLB-473 | Tricopter wiring blocks must be BATTERY, XT60 + CAP "1000 uF 35 V", 3x ESC "30 A each, BLHeli_S", FLIGHT CTRL "F4 + PDB, TRI mixer", RECEIVER "ELRS 2.4 GHz", TAIL SERVO "metal gear, 5 V", CAMERA "analog, 15 deg", VTX "5.8 GHz, 400 mW" and MOTORS x3, at the listed positions. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2717-2731 | must | e2e |

| DLB-477 | must not copy: the tricopter angle snippet's "set p_level = 50" / "set i_level = 50" (removed in Betaflight 4.5); it must use angle_p_gain and keep angle_limit = 38 to match the simulator's 38°. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2751-2757; §3 L3297-3320 | must | review |
| DLB-478 | must not copy: the tricopter acro comment "~540 deg/s at full stick" for roll_rc_rate 100 / roll_srate 70; the snippet must state rates_type and the rate it really gives (about 667°/s with BETAFLIGHT rates, 700°/s maximum with ACTUAL), keeping p_roll = 45 and d_roll = 40. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2761-2768; §3 L3300 | must | review |
| DLB-479 | must not copy: the tricopter race snippet's "set osd_timer_1 = ON" and "set osd_stat_max_spd = ON"; Betaflight's timer settings are osd_tim1 and osd_tim_1_pos and its stats are chosen with osd_stat_bitmask. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2772-2777; §3 L3314 | must | review |

| DLB-481 | The yaw-demo snippet must keep mixer TRI, tri_unarmed_servo = ON, yaw_motors_reversed = OFF, p_yaw = 60 and "smix reverse 5 2 r" with the note that on a tri yaw is servo tilt, not motor torque. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2781-2789 | must | review |
| DLB-482 | The yaw demo must arm itself on the ground, hold 1.6 m (throttle stick clamp(1.4 · (1.6 − z))), yaw with stick 0.85 · sin(1.1 t) and damp the pitch and roll sticks by 0.9 per step. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2794-2795 | must | unit |
| DLB-483 | The tail servo angle must target 30° × yaw stick while armed, easing toward clamp(target, ±40°) at min(1, 14 · dt), and show as the "Tail tilt" n° flight stat. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2796-2797, L2850 | must | unit |
| DLB-484 | must not copy: a cosmetic tail servo (yaw from the stock 200°/s torque-style yaw, tilt only drawn) and a scripted pirouette; tricopter yaw must come from the tail motor's thrust tilted by the servo angle, with a tail-torque trim, so a reversed servo diverges from the physics itself. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2802-2803, L2885-2886 | must | unit |
| DLB-485 | A reversed tail servo must start a runaway pirouette soon after take-off (the original: 0.2 s, 60°/s growing 700°/s² to 1100°/s, servo slamming between its stops) with "Pirouette! | DLB-486 | must not copy: a pirouette that never ends with no crash or recovery; a reversed-servo flight must end (crash or the pilot disarming), matching "nothing else can stop the yaw". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2807-2808, L2857, L2890 | must | e2e |
| DLB-487 | The yaw demo must reproduce the original: it arms with "Hands off: watch the tail motor tilt to yaw", climbs to 1.6 m (about 44 % throttle) and swings its heading about ±150° while the tail tilts up to ±25°. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2805-2806 | must | e2e |

| DLB-491 | The park must hold footpaths, a pond (r 4.2 m) at (8, 24), the fountain at (36, 28) (stone basin r 2.5 m, non-solid water, 1.7 m pillar, bowl at 1.6 m), six 3.9 m lamp posts at x 8-38 on y 16, the gazebo at (14, 32) (four 2.6 m posts, slab, pyramid roof), four benches, a big oak at (24, 39), five trees and a playground at (40, 41). | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2817-2821 | must | unit |
| DLB-492 | The park checkpoints must be the rings SLALOM 1-5 at (11, 14.6), (17, 17.4), (23, 14.6), (29, 17.4), (35, 14.6) (1.5 m, r 0.9, heading 0°), the spheres ORBIT S (36, 23), E (41, 28), N (36, 33), W (31, 28) (2 m, r 1.4), the ring GAZEBO (14, 32, 1.3, r 0.8, heading 180°) and the sphere LAND (23, 6, 0.8, r 1.6), in order. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2823-2835 | must | unit |
| DLB-493 | The park range code must keep tri_unarmed_servo = ON, yaw_rc_rate = 90, p_yaw = 60 and i_yaw = 90 with their comments on smooth yaw. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2837-2846 | must | review |
| DLB-494 | The tricopter controls card must be the engine default (W S throttle / alt hold, A D yaw, arrows pitch + roll, Space arm / disarm, R reset, V FPV, plus the drag line), with FPV tilted up 22°. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2848-2850 | must | e2e |
| DLB-495 | "Meet the tricopter" must explain the Y of three motors with a tail motor on a servo, smooth plane-like yaw against a quad's torque yaw, good for video and efficient with big props, and the price: the servo works all flight and if it stalls, slips or is mounted backwards nothing else can stop the yaw. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2852-2857 | must | review |
| DLB-496 | The tricopter build CLI must keep mixer TRI (M1 tail, M2 front right, M3 front left), tri_unarmed_servo = ON, "servo 5 1000 2000 1500 100 -1" (5 = tail), "smix reverse 5 2 r", align_board_yaw = 0, p_yaw = 60 and save. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2859-2870 | must | review |
| DLB-497 | The tricopter practice task must ask to fly the yaw demo, do slow 360° pirouettes in altitude hold keeping the tail tilt well under 40°, and on a real tri centre the servo with props off, check yaw right tilts the tail motor left, then hover and trim until it holds heading hands-off. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2872-2874 | must | review |
| DLB-498 | Tricopter quiz 1 must ask "How does a tricopter yaw?" with the right answer "A servo tilts the tail motor so part of its thrust pushes the tail sideways". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2878 | must | e2e |
| DLB-499 | Tricopter quiz 2 must ask "Why do the two front props spin in opposite directions?" with the right answer "To cancel each other's torque, so the servo only trims the tail's leftover". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2879 | must | e2e |
| DLB-500 | Tricopter quiz 3 must ask "You take off and the tri instantly starts spinning faster and faster. First suspect?" with the right answer "The tail servo is reversed: it tilts the wrong way and adds to the yaw". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2880 | must | e2e |
| DLB-501 | Tricopter quiz 4 must ask "Which single part fails most on a tri?" with the right answer "The tail servo (gears, linkage, power)". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 12 L2881 | must | e2e |

| DLB-550 | The film-set checkpoints must be the rings TRACK 1 (38, 39, heading 32.01°), TRACK 2 (46, 44, 54.46°), TRACK 3 (52, 52.4, 54.46°), TRACK 4 (57.2, 61.2, 69.44°) at 5 m (r 2.6), the spheres ORBIT 1-4 at (51.07, 70.16), (56.34, 58.57), (67.93, 63.84), (62.66, 75.43) (5 m, r 2.4, 9 m from the car at 90° steps), REVEAL (92, 42, 17, r 3.5) and LAND AT BASE (22, 18, 1, r 2.4), in order. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3167-3181 | must | unit |
| DLB-551 | must not copy: the film-set mission comment's placeholders (`<home>`, `<road 1..4>`, `<hero car>`, `<mesa>`); the shot list must be a complete QGC WPL 110 mission with real coordinates. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3183-3190; SPEC.md L123 | must | review |
| DLB-552 | must not copy: command 34 labelled LOITER_TURNS; 34 is MAV_CMD_DO_ORBIT, and the orbit must use NAV_LOITER_TURNS (18) with turns 2, heading 1, radius 9 m and xtrack 0. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3188, L3242-3243 | must | review |
| DLB-553 | must not copy: MNT1_TYPE 4 (SToRM32 over MAVLink) while the part text and wiring say the gimbal talks over CAN; the mount type, part text and wiring must name the same gimbal interface. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3037, L3065, L3191, L3244 | must | review |
| DLB-554 | The octo controls card must read W S "climb / descend (height is held)", A D "yaw", arrows "move (loiter: let go to stop)", Space "arm / disarm", R "reset to the pad", plus "or drag the two sticks on the figure". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3194-3196 | must | e2e |
| DLB-555 | "Meet the octo" must explain that the design goal is not falling with a camera worth more than the aircraft, the seven motors rebalancing after one dies while a quad that loses one of four falls, flat octo versus X8 coax (coax folds smaller but loses about 15-20 % in the upper props' wash), 12S carrying the same power at half the current, the gimbal, and legs retracting for 360° pans. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3198-3205 | must | review |
| DLB-556 | The octo build params must keep FRAME_CLASS 3 (octa), FRAME_TYPE 1 (X), MOT_THST_EXPO 0.70, MOT_SPIN_ARM 0.08, MOT_SPIN_MIN 0.12, BATT_MONITOR 4, BATT_CAPACITY 16000, BATT_LOW_VOLT 42.0, BATT_FS_LOW_ACT 2 and SERVO9_FUNCTION 29 (landing gear), with LGR_DEPLOY_ALT given in metres. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3207-3222; §3 L3297-3320 | must | review |
| DLB-557 | The octo practice task must ask to run the motor-failure test, note the throttle before and after, work out the new hover throttle by hand for seven motors, then design an octo that cannot survive it by choosing the 6S pack. | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3224-3226 | must | review |
| DLB-558 | Octo quiz 1 must ask "Why can an octocopter survive a motor failure when a quad cannot?" with the right answer "The other seven can still balance thrust and torque; a quad loses control of an axis". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3230 | must | e2e |
| DLB-559 | Octo quiz 2 must ask "Why fly a 15 kg lifter on 12S instead of 6S?" with the right answer "Same power at double the voltage means half the current: cooler wires and ESCs". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3231 | must | e2e |
| DLB-560 | Octo quiz 3 must ask "Why do the landing legs fold up after take-off?" with the right answer "So the camera can pan 360 degrees without the legs in shot". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3232 | must | e2e |
| DLB-561 | Octo quiz 4 must ask "X8 coaxial vs flat octo: what does the coax lose?" with the right answer "~15-20% efficiency: the lower props run in the upper props' wash". | drone-lab-b.md Fig 13 L3233 | must | e2e |

## CF · Look, palette, ink and paper

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CF-001 | Every board, component, schematic, wiring diagram, exploded view and drone must be drawn with the Colorfigures engine; a hand-drawn SVG is allowed only where the skill fails, with the reason recorded in docs/DECISIONS.md. | SPEC.md L16, L122 | must | review |
| CF-002 | Every figure must look hand-inked and isometric: the #hand filter's warp noise drives a displacement wobble (default scale 2.4 × INK_K) with a fine grain and a coarse blot multiplied in, on warm paper (--paper #f2efe7). | SPEC.md L21; colorfigures-guide.md §1.9 L218-225, §1.10 L288 | must | screenshot |
| CF-003 | Figures must start at the true isometric view (yaw 45°, pitch 35.264°), where one model unit projects to one viewBox unit. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.1 L62-65, §1.3 L99, L117-118 | must | unit |
| CF-004 | Bodies must be shaded in three flat tones (light, mid, dark) chosen by how each face meets a light fixed to the viewer, with soft bands so faces fade rather than snap while turning. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.6 L164-176; SKILL.md L10-11, L99-100 | must | screenshot |
| CF-005 | Every figure must have a heavy ink silhouette (hullInk about 2.8) with finer inner lines (panels about 2.2, openings 1.6-1.8, details 0.8-1.4), all in INK #231e16, the only dark stroke colour. | SKILL.md L105-106, L113; colorfigures-guide.md §1.2 L83 | must | screenshot |
| CF-006 | Every object must cast a short flat blue shadow (SHADOW #41538f) connected to it, clipped to its board when it stands on one, and tall things on a thin support must cast base, support and high part so the shadows touch. | SKILL.md L109-111; colorfigures-guide.md §1.2 L84, §1.7 L198 | must | screenshot |
| CF-007 | Body colours must come from the five palettes exactly: tomato {#ef6a4c, #cf5038, #4a2219}, mustard {#f4b829, #d49c1f, #4e4a18}, mint {#93d3b0, #71b693, #1f4034}, cobalt {#76a0e2, #5a82c6, #1c2948} and blush {#f3a6bb, #d8869c, #4b2430}. | SPEC.md L28; colorfigures-guide.md §1.2 L75-81 | must | unit |
| CF-008 | The technical monochrome must be the core materials METAL, CLEAR, CERAMIC, DARK, BOARD and WOOD (plus matOf of a six-digit hex for anything else), with accents used sparingly: status lights #43c25c / #e2483d, screens #1d2a26 with #8af0ae text, glass #cfe9f0 at low opacity with one or two white streaks. | SPEC.md L28; colorfigures-guide.md §1.2 L82-85; SKILL.md L99-100, L112-113 | must | review |
| CF-009 | A figure's palette (st.pal) must always be one of the five PALS keys; custom body materials must live in the figure's own state. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.2 L87-88 | must | unit |
| CF-010 | A table-top object must stand on a board drawn first and included in size; a floor-standing machine must stand on the paper. | SKILL.md L107-108 | must | screenshot |
| CF-011 | Figures must be neat and slender like the real thing: repeated hairlines 8-18, thin parts thin, attached parts touching their body at both ends, every detail big enough to read at normal size. | SKILL.md L114-116 | must | screenshot |
| CF-012 | Empty space in a figure should hold things from the scene's story (props on the board or mat), sorted and shadowed like the rest. | SKILL.md L115-116 | should | screenshot |
| CF-013 | Text drawn in a figure must use plain letters, digits and ordinary punctuation; emoji and pictographic symbols must not be drawn, since they render as blobs. | SKILL.md L117 | must | review |
| CF-014 | The hand-inked look must hold on phones and touch screens too, or docs/DECISIONS.md must record the exception: build.py removes the #hand filter on narrow or touch screens at all times, although its docstring promises only a lighter filter while moving. | SPEC.md L21; colorfigures-guide.md §2.1 L394; §5 L1032; Appendix E L1516 | must | screenshot |
| CF-015 | The schematic, breadboard and wiring views must use the same hand-inked look (ink filter, wobble, paper grain, the five palettes) and come from the same data as the 3D figures, also when the host renders them. | SPEC.md L154; colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L795-798, §3.5 L894-898 | must | screenshot |
| CF-016 | The originals' figure controls (hand-drawn wobble slider 0-7, colour swatches per palette, labels on / off, plus the skill's Front view and Turntable) must stay available, in the figure or in the host through the bridge, when embedded mode hides the controls row. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L288-291, §2.3 L447-448, §3.2 L641 | must | e2e |
| CF-017 | The paper grain frequency must scale with the zoom, as build.py's zoom does, so the grain stays fine when zoomed in. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.1 L398 | must | screenshot |

## CF · Rotation, camera and drawing order

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CF-018 | Every figure must ship with turn:'full' so a drag turns it all the way round (360°), once every angle has passed verification. | SPEC.md L22; SKILL.md L62-63; colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L238, §4.6 L1002 | must | e2e |
| CF-019 | Dragging anywhere off a part must orbit the camera (yaw += dx · 0.55 · k, pitch += dy · 0.35 · k with k = 300 / figure width, pointer captured, so a full-width drag on a 760 px figure turns about 165°), with pitch clamped to 6-78° and yaw never wrapped. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.3 L106, §1.10 L283-285 | must | e2e |
| CF-020 | Double-clicking off a part must ease back to the front view in 700 ms. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L284-285 | must | e2e |
| CF-021 | Arrow keys on a focused figure must turn it (Left / Right −15° / +15°, Up / Down pitch +8° / −8°, each a 260 ms eased move), except while game keys are active or the target is a text input. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L286-287 | must | e2e |
| CF-022 | The view controls must include "Front view" (which also stops the turntable) and "Turntable" (18°/s). | colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L289-290 | must | e2e |
| CF-023 | ?yaw, ?pitch and ?free=1 must set the view for screenshots, and a camera move requested by the host must ease the shortest way round in yaw and cancel any running move or drag. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.3 L115, §1.10 L293-294; SKILL.md L60-61 | must | e2e |
| CF-024 | The light must stay fixed relative to the viewer while the cast shadow follows only the yaw. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.6 L173-176; §1.3 L109 | must | screenshot |
| CF-025 | A figure must be correct from every side, back and underside included: nothing painted over a nearer part, no missing face, nothing floating, no mirrored text, nothing visible through a solid wall. | SKILL.md L16, L178-180; colorfigures-guide.md §4.6 L995-996 | must | screenshot |
| CF-026 | Parts with no single correct order must be drawn in both orders by which side faces the viewer, and things that wrap others must be split far half, contents, near half (openShell, nearHalf / farHalf), never a hard-coded visible half. | SKILL.md L78-81 | must | screenshot |
| CF-027 | Real objects must have backs (service panels, cords, vents, a chassis underneath), and parts seen only from behind must keep real proportions and exist from every side. | SKILL.md L89-91 | must | screenshot |
| CF-028 | Contents seen through an opening must be real 3D objects behind the glass, clipped to the opening and centred in it as seen straight on, checked numerically. | SKILL.md L92-93 | must | review |
| CF-029 | After a part is resized or moved, the order of everything it now overlaps must be re-checked, and flat floor items may go first only after the cast shadows. | SKILL.md L95-96 | must | review |
| CF-030 | must not copy: the originals' 90 ms interval that renders while powered even when nothing changes, or the old core's separate 16 ms loop per animate call; figures must render from the one shared frame driver only while something moves. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.1 L590, L625; §3.2 L642; §1.10 L272-273 | must | unit |

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| CF-032 | The internal mechanisms in See-through must be labelled, animated and lit in the accent colour while each part is working. | SPEC.md L23; SKILL.md L18, L135-138 | must | e2e |
| CF-033 | The See-through and Labels switches must start off, so the closed object is what people see first, and the closed view must be the real object with no cutaway. | SKILL.md L121-122, L130 | must | e2e |
| CF-034 | See-through must fade in and out (st.xr eased toward its target at 1 / 0.7 per second), with the far inside walls faint, the case as a ghost and the mechanism at opacity k drawn over the ghost. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.12 L317-325; SKILL.md L123-126 | must | e2e |
| CF-035 | Inside parts and every label must be drawn above the ghosted shell, never inside it; whatever covers the mechanism (glass, doors, collars) must fade too, with handles and knobs on it redrawn on top. | SKILL.md L127-129 | must | screenshot |
| CF-036 | Anything the case hid partly must be drawn whole in See-through, and motions that relied on a clip must get real limits. | SKILL.md L131-132 | must | screenshot |
| CF-037 | A see-through mechanism must be built in 3D (not a flat picture on a plate): long parts through a ring drawn in pieces either side, bearing plates as brackets, wires as short pieces that sort with the solids. | SKILL.md L133-134 | must | screenshot |
| CF-038 | A see-through should show 30-60 real parts (motors, gears, shafts, springs, boards, sensors, wiring, tubes, brackets) for a machine of that complexity. | SKILL.md L44-46 | should | review |
| CF-039 | In See-through, labels must be xrCallouts pills in two columns either side of the object, sorted by screen height and pushed apart so leaders never cross, drawn last and only once the fade passes 0.6; outside See-through, surface parts must be labelled with tag pills. | SKILL.md L135-138; colorfigures-guide.md §1.12 L316, L323-325 | must | screenshot |
| CF-040 | Labels must be short names in capitals (for Latin, Greek and Cyrillic scripts), must name only parts that are drawn, and must be anchored on their part and move with it. | SKILL.md L137-138; colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L548 | must | review |
| CF-041 | Label pills must not overlap each other or leave the viewBox at any verified angle. | colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L956-961 | must | screenshot |
| CF-042 | On a phone each label must become a numbered badge, and a two-column list of the numbered names must appear under the figure (accent when on), renumbered 1..N whenever the label set changes; tapping a name must highlight its badge. | SKILL.md L139-140; colorfigures-guide.md §1.7 L202, §2.1 L401 | must | e2e |
| CF-043 | The Labels switch must work on its own, and ?xr=1 / ?labels=1 must open See-through and labels for screenshots. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L293-294, §1.12 L321 | must | e2e |
| CF-044 | Each board part kind must have its own see-through drawing where it has a real inside (die with bond wires in a QFP or DIP, the crystal can's quartz blank, the USB bridge, the polyfuse), drawn over a ghosted board with callouts that light while that part works. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L740, L783-786 | must | screenshot |
| CF-045 | Every drone figure must have a See-through with labelled working parts, although the original drone core had none (which parts each craft shows is gap G04). | SPEC.md L23; colorfigures-guide.md §0 L43-48 | must | screenshot |

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
