| CF-050 | Buttons must be the figure's interface, 2-5 meaningful actions in the order a person would use them, with pickers marked by "▾" whose current chip flashes "{label} — already set" and whose disabled chip shows its why text or "{label} isn't possible right now". | SKILL.md L143-145; colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L253-257 | must | e2e |
| CF-051 | Out-of-order presses must do something sensible: run the missing step or show a notice on the figure that says what to do first. | SKILL.md L148-149 | must | e2e |
| CF-052 | Notices must sit on the figure, centred 6 % from the top on #fff1bf with a round "!" badge and a capitalised first letter, shown for 1600 ms by default, a later notice replacing an earlier one. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.1 L399, §1.10 L274, §4.3 L967-969 | must | screenshot |
| CF-053 | Every supply in a figure must come from something in the scene. | SKILL.md L147 | must | review |
| CF-054 | Measured quantities must show as stats in the top right, a changed value bumping for 0.35 s. | SKILL.md L148-149; colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L245 | must | e2e |
| CF-055 | Animations must be eased, mostly under about 2 s, and safe when another button is pressed meanwhile (one state object). | SKILL.md L150-151 | must | e2e |
| CF-056 | Game-style figures must use mountKeypad (an inked pad with role group and aria-label "Keypad", 34 px keys or 44 px on a phone, Enter for a 120 ms press, keys lit while held) with window.GAME_KEYS set so the arrows play instead of turning the camera and Space always triggers the action; on a phone the pad must become one slim row ordered ◀ ▲ ▼ ▶. | SKILL.md L152-154; colorfigures-guide.md §1.13 L329-335 | must | e2e |
| CF-057 | Every interesting state must be reachable from URL params read in params(st, q) so a screenshot can show it. | SKILL.md L155; colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L247 | must | e2e |
| CF-058 | The figure page must carry no hint line, captions or explanation text: words live on buttons and labels, and lessons, quiz, code and logs live in the host. | SKILL.md L156; colorfigures-guide.md §3.1 L626 | must | review |
| CF-059 | The zoom chip "−" / "100%" / "+" (aria "Zoom out", "Reset zoom", "Zoom in") must zoom 1× to 5× by ×1.5 per click, with Ctrl / Cmd + wheel zooming at the pointer, plain wheel panning only while zoomed, and two-finger pinch and pan on touch. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.1 L398 | must | e2e |

## CF · Truth first: real-world geometry and labelling

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CF-060 | Every figure must model the real object with accurate real-world geometry and labelling. | SPEC.md L26; SKILL.md L38-40 | must | review |
| CF-061 | Every figure script must begin with how the real thing works as a short list of steps (where every material is after each one) and a parts list grouped as case, structure, mechanism, power and controls, written before any geometry. | SKILL.md L30-31, L41-46; colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L804 | must | review |
| CF-062 | Each button must be one real step, and things must move only along their real path. | SKILL.md L41-43 | must | e2e |
| CF-063 | Proportions the author is unsure of must be looked up, and the figure's dimensions must come from datasheets or measured drawings. | SKILL.md L45-46; colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L810-811 | must | review |
| CF-064 | Every part must be held by another (name what it is fixed to); nothing floats, and cables and tubes run from a real port to a real port along walls and floor, never across open space. | SKILL.md L47-48; colorfigures-guide.md §4.6 L999 | must | screenshot |
| CF-065 | Every driving part must touch what it drives (shaft to gear, rim to rim, pipe to port), with the contact points worked out in numbers. | SKILL.md L49-50; colorfigures-guide.md §4.6 L999 | must | review |
| CF-066 | Moving paths must be laid out in numbers so they clear every gear and shaft, and things moving into a container go up and over the rim, then down inside. | SKILL.md L51-52 | must | review |
| CF-067 | Things people use must be scaled against people (a push button at about 1.1 m, facing where people stand). | SKILL.md L53 | must | review |
| CF-068 | Model units must follow the skill's scale (about 1 unit per cm, small objects modelled larger at 60-100 units) with x as depth, y as width (+y viewer-left) and z up from ground 0, so real dimensions read off the model. | SKILL.md L57-59; colorfigures-guide.md §1.1 L60-65 | must | review |
| CF-069 | Drone figures must draw real part sizes from their codes ("2306" is a 23 mm × 6 mm stator; "5x4.3x3" is 5 in diameter, 4.3 in pitch, 3 blades) and real frame layouts (x, stretch-x, h, plus, tri, hex, octo, whoop, wing) with the wheelbase measured motor to motor on the diagonal. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.3 L663, §3.5 L852-853 | must | unit |
| CF-070 | Board figures must place holes, silk, copper, pins and parts in millimetres measured from the left and front edges of the real PCB, with pin anchors shared by jumpers, breadboard legs and the schematic. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L731-736 | must | unit |

## CF · Self-contained pages, vendored engine, licence and phone layout

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CF-071 | Every built figure page must be self-contained HTML, SVG and JS: the engine, bridge, SL helpers, engine files, figure file and its data are all inlined, and the page makes no outside requests (no CDN scripts, web fonts, remote images or analytics). | SPEC.md L27; colorfigures-guide.md §2.1 L374-377, §3.3 L673-676, L682-684; ~/.claude/skills/colorfigures/README.md L37 | must | unit |
| CF-072 | The figure builder must inline every local script src (build.py inlines only core.js and leaves other script tags external), add build.py's HEAD and TAIL, refuse inlined code containing `</script` and fail on a missing file. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.1 L374-377, §3.3 L653, L673-676 | must | unit |
| CF-073 | The builder should prove, with a --check run, that a core-only page comes out byte-identical to build.py's output. | colorfigures-guide.md §4.2 L925-934 | should | unit |
| CF-074 | Every figure page must load its scripts in the order core.js, lib/embed.js (which must wrap mountFigure before anything calls it), lib/sl.js, the engine files, the figure file, then the one line that starts it. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.3 L674-675 | must | unit |
| CF-075 | The engine should be a vendored copy of the skill (figures/vendor/colorfigures with core.js, build.py, VERSION holding the skill date and sha256, LICENSE and PATCHES.md), and every change to the vendored core should be recorded in PATCHES.md. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.3 L652; §2.3 L546-547 | should | review |
| CF-076 | SimLab must keep the Colorfigures MIT licence with its copyright lines (April Zhu for Colorfigures and iso-glow, Tolga Cohce for iso-figure) in the vendored copy. | SPEC.md L155; colorfigures-guide.md §2.2 L428-429; ~/.claude/skills/colorfigures/LICENSE | must | review |
| CF-077 | Because embedded mode hides the figure's "Made with Colorfigures ↗" footer, an About page must credit Colorfigures by April Zhu with a link to https://april-zhu.com/colorfigures, plus the skill's own credits (ISO-GLOW by April Zhu, iso-figure by Tolga Cohce, the see-through method of Anatomy by Ryan, all MIT). | SPEC.md L155; colorfigures-guide.md §2.2 L428-429, §2.3 L447; ~/.claude/skills/colorfigures/README.md L39-43 | must | e2e |
| CF-078 | Each board and each craft should be its own built page (about 150 kB: core, build extras, engine, figure), loaded with a `?v=<build hash>` cache buster. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.3 L678-680 | should | unit |
| CF-079 | Built figure pages must be served same-origin by Next.js from public/figures/, without X-Frame-Options DENY or a frame-ancestors 'none' policy on that path. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L520, L526-528 | must | e2e |
| CF-080 | At 560 px wide or less (the iframe's own width) a figure must switch to the phone layout: header hidden, buttons in a wrapping row, the taller frame (FIG_A 1.1), 20 px stat numbers, labels as numbered badges with the name list underneath, and the keypad as one slim row. | SPEC.md L128; colorfigures-guide.md §2.1 L379-401, §2.2 L412 | must | screenshot |
| CF-081 | On touch screens a one-finger horizontal drag must turn the figure, a vertical swipe must scroll the page (touch-action pan-y), and two fingers must zoom and pan the figure, not the page. | SPEC.md L128; colorfigures-guide.md §1.9 L219-220, §2.1 L398, §2.2 L422-423 | must | e2e |
| CF-082 | Phone figure controls must be simplified (build.py hides the wobble slider on narrow or touch screens) while every action stays reachable. | SPEC.md L128; colorfigures-guide.md §2.1 L394 | must | e2e |
| CF-083 | At phone width a figure page must not scroll sideways and nothing in it may be cut off. | SPEC.md L128; colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L956-957, §4.6 L1000 | must | screenshot |
| CF-084 | A standalone (not embedded) figure page should keep build.py's fit() on desktop, capping the figure so the whole page fits the window, never below 320 px. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.1 L397, §2.2 L425-427 | should | screenshot |
| CF-085 | The host lesson must say what each figure button and pressable part does, since the figure page itself shows no instructions. | SKILL.md L35-36, L156 | must | review |

## CF · Embedded mode and the figure bridge contract

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CF-086 | A figure must switch to embedded mode inside any iframe, with ?embed=1 forcing it on and ?embed=0 forcing it off. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L436-440 | must | e2e |
| CF-087 | Embedded mode must hide the figure's header, controls row, credit footer and phone label list (the host draws the list from the labels event) and keep the zoom chip and the notice on the figure. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L447-449, L459-466 | must | screenshot |
| CF-088 | ?bare=1 should also hide the figure's buttons, stats, switches and keypad, for pages where the host draws those controls. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L441, L447-448, L470 | should | screenshot |
| CF-089 | A figure must accept ?lang=xx for the language of its first paint and ?theme=dark for the dimmed paper. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L442-443, L536-537, L553 | must | e2e |
| CF-090 | A figure should accept ?origin=https://… naming the host origin for the case where it is served from another origin. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L444 | should | unit |
| CF-091 | Every interesting figure state must be reachable from URL params (id, module, sheet, xr=1, labels=1, yaw, pitch and the figure's own params keys), so a lesson link or a screenshot can open it. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L445; SKILL.md L155 | must | e2e |
| CF-092 | In embedded mode the SVG must fill the iframe (width and height 100 %, beating fit()) and the figure page must not scroll. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L451-452, L460-465 | must | screenshot |
| CF-093 | The host must give each figure iframe a definite box (aspect-ratio 4 / 3 on desktop, about 10 / 11 on phones, max-height about 80vh), and the bridge must set FIG_A to clamp(innerHeight / innerWidth, .7501, 1.4) before mount so the frame matches the iframe. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L452-455; §5 L1030 | must | screenshot |
| CF-094 | Host-to-figure messages must use the envelope {source: 'simlab', v: 1, type, …} and figure-to-host messages {source: 'simlab-figure', v: 1, type, …}; messages with any other source or version must be ignored. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L474-475; Appendix D L1454-1455, L1491-1492 | must | unit |
| CF-095 | The figure must answer every hello with ready, idempotently. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L479, L515-516 | must | e2e |
| CF-096 | press {id, choice?} must click a figure button by its stable id (by default a slug of the English label, such as push-the-lever) and pick a picker chip by id, in any language. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L480, L538-539 | must | e2e |
| CF-097 | act {name, data?} must run cfg.acts[name] exactly as if that part had been pressed. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L481 | must | e2e |
| CF-098 | set {state} must pass the patch to the figure's accept(st, patch), which checks types, ranges and the current phase; no other state can be written from outside (set {shade: 0, pal: 'nonsense'} applies only the shade). | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L482, L505-506, L524-525; §3.1 L627 | must | unit |
| CF-099 | view {yaw, pitch?, ms?} should move the camera with ui.camTo. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L483, L504 | should | e2e |
| CF-100 | xr {on} and labels {on} must flip the See-through and Labels switches only when they differ from the requested state. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L484, L504-505 | must | e2e |
| CF-101 | pal {key} must change the palette, accepting only PALS keys. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L485; §1.2 L87 | must | unit |
| CF-102 | locale {lang, strings?} must swap the figure's dictionary, relabel its buttons and switches and re-render the drawing. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L486, L506-508 | must | e2e |
| CF-103 | theme {mode} must dim the paper when mode is 'dark' and restore it otherwise. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L487, L553-554 | must | e2e |
| CF-104 | flash {text, ms?} should show a notice on the figure. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L488 | should | e2e |
| CF-105 | query should make the figure answer with a state event. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L489, L512-514 | should | e2e |
| CF-106 | The figure must post ready {fig, title, lang, buttons [{id, label, picker}], acts} at mount, not from a timer, and after every hello. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L493, L515-516; Appendix E L1523 | must | e2e |
| CF-107 | A press on a drawn part must post part {act, id, data} with all of the part's data-* attributes. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L494, L503 | must | e2e |
| CF-108 | Every button, chip or switch click must post button {id, choice?, by}, with by 'user' or 'host'. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L495, L502-503 | must | e2e |
| CF-109 | After any render in which report(st) changed, the figure must post state {state, changed}. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L496 | must | e2e |
| CF-110 | report(st) must return discrete values only (phase, power, pins, fault, mode); continuous values must be rounded or left out, so the bridge never posts every frame (the toaster's brown value posted 60 messages a second). | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L512-514; §3.4 L743; Appendix C L1298, L1443 | must | unit |
| CF-111 | Whenever the visible labels change, the figure must post labels {items [{n, text, on}]} so the host can draw the label list. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L497, L504-505 | must | e2e |
| CF-112 | Every notice shown on the figure must also be posted as flash {text, en} so the host can mirror it into the lab log. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L498 | must | e2e |
| CF-113 | The figure should post focus {on} when it gains or loses keyboard focus. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L499, L558 | should | e2e |
| CF-114 | Uncaught figure errors, unknown press ids and unknown act names must be posted as error {message} (press nope and act ghost both answer error) as well as written to data-errors. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L500, L505; §2.2 L430; §1.1 L58-59 | must | e2e |
| CF-115 | The host must post hello on the iframe's load event and queue every other command until it sees ready, and every ready (first load or a reload) must flush the queue. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L515-516; Appendix D L1480-1497 | must | unit |
| CF-116 | The host must never reset readiness in onLoad, because under CPU throttling the figure's ready arrives before load (board.html at 12× throttle: ready 450 ms, load 454 ms; at 20×: 726 / 734 ms). | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L516-519; Appendix D L1503; Appendix E L1518 | must | e2e |
| CF-117 | Both sides must post to the real origin (location.origin) and check ev.source (window.parent in the figure, the iframe's contentWindow in the host) and ev.origin before acting; '*' is allowed only for file:// tests, and a message the iframe posts to itself must be ignored. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L506, L520-523; Appendix D L1483, L1490; Appendix E L1519 | must | unit |
| CF-118 | Figure iframes should carry no sandbox attribute (allow-scripts with allow-same-origin gives no isolation, and without allow-same-origin the origin turns opaque) and rely on the strict message checks instead. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L526-528 | should | review |
| CF-119 | The host must not put overlays on top of a figure iframe, since a drag started on the figure keeps turning it past the iframe edge through pointer capture. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.2 L419-421 | must | e2e |

## CF · Localisation, dark mode, keyboard focus and hidden iframes

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CF-120 | Every figure string (label pills, on-face text, notices, button and chip labels) must have translations in English, Spanish, French, German, Chinese and Hindi and show in the language the host has chosen. | SPEC.md L127; colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L530-535 | must | e2e |
| CF-121 | Figures must look every string up through one function, CF_T(englishText), in CF_STRINGS[lang], keyed by the English source text so an untranslated string still reads in English. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L532-533 | must | unit |
| CF-122 | The bridge must translate button and chip labels itself, figures must call CF_T for label pills, on-face text and ui.flash messages, and the bridge must also translate any notice whose text matches a key. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L533-535 | must | unit |
| CF-123 | The host's i18n (src/lib/i18n.tsx) must be the single source of figure translations: it must send locale {lang, strings} after ready and on every language change, and pass ?lang=xx so the first paint is already in the chosen language. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L536-537 | must | e2e |
| CF-124 | A language change must relabel the figure's buttons and switches and re-render its drawing in place (tested: "Elige el tostado▾", "Baja la palanca", "Saca la tostada", "Por dentro", "Etiquetas"), and a disabled chip must flash its translated why text. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L506-508 | must | e2e |
| CF-125 | The core's two notice patterns, "{label} — already set" and "{label} isn't possible right now", must be translated by a pattern in the bridge's flash wrapper. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L540-542; Appendix C L1442-1443 | must | e2e |
| CF-126 | Core strings that cannot be translated without a patch (the controls row, the credit, the keypad's "SPACE") must stay hidden in embedded mode or be translated by a patch recorded in PATCHES.md. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L540-542, L447 | must | e2e |
| CF-127 | Figures must stay a paper plate in both themes: body colours, ink, the cream label pills, the ghost tint (hard-coded toward #f2efe7 in xrGhost) and the blue shadow must never be inverted for dark mode. | SPEC.md L66; colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L551-553 | must | screenshot |
| CF-128 | In dark mode the figure must dim its paper to --paper #e4ddcf, --rule #cbc2b1 and --muted #6a6357 (the page background becomes rgb(228, 221, 207)), framed as a plate in the dark host. | SPEC.md L66; colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L471, L553-554 | must | screenshot |
| CF-129 | The host must send theme on ready and on every theme change, because prefers-color-scheme inside the iframe follows the operating system rather than the host's toggle. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L556 | must | e2e |
| CF-130 | Each figure iframe element must have the paper colour as its background (dimmed in dark mode) and color-scheme: light, so there is no white flash and form controls stay light. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L555; Appendix D L1506-1507 | must | screenshot |
| CF-131 | Each figure iframe must carry a title naming the figure, and the figure's #fig must carry an aria-label from its title. | SPEC.md L129; colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L237; Appendix D L1451, L1506 | must | review |
| CF-132 | The bridge must make #fig keyboard-focusable (tabindex="0") with a visible focus ring (2 px solid ink, offset −3 px), so keyboard users can Tab into a figure and turn it with the arrow keys. | SPEC.md L129; colorfigures-guide.md §2.2 L416-418, §2.3 L466, L558 | must | e2e |
| CF-133 | The See-through and Labels switches must be real buttons with aria-pressed, and every figure control must be reachable and usable from the keyboard. | SPEC.md L129; colorfigures-guide.md §1.12 L321, §1.13 L332-333 | must | e2e |
| CF-134 | When the user starts using a lab, the host should focus the figure iframe (iframe.contentWindow.focus()) or forward keys to it, since arrow keys reach a figure only while its document has focus. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.2 L416-418, §2.3 L558-560 | should | e2e |
| CF-135 | Figure clocks must run on the dt from ui.animate, not on performance.now(), so a hidden or background iframe slows down instead of jumping ahead. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L562-565 | must | unit |
| CF-136 | The bridge should add pause and resume commands so the host can stop a hidden figure from animating. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L564-565; Appendix C L1443-1444 | should | e2e |

## CF · Verification recipe

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CF-137 | Before any SimLab figure is built, the vendored engine and the shot suite must be proven on the skill's toaster reference (25 shots, 0 errors, 9 see-through labels with no overlaps and none off-frame at every angle). | SPEC.md L133; colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L938-943, L961-962 | must | screenshot |
| CF-138 | Every figure must be iterated until its see-through internals, labels, animations and interactions are correct; the brief's "iterate until correct" overrides the skill's three-round cap. | SPEC.md L141; SKILL.md L34 | must | review |
| CF-139 | Every figure must be shot at the seven angles free=1 with yaw=45, 110, 170, 230, 300, yaw=20&pitch=62 and yaw=200&pitch=70, closed. | SKILL.md L173-180; colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L950 | must | screenshot |
| CF-140 | Every figure must be shot at the same seven angles with &xr=1&labels=1. | SKILL.md L178; colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L951 | must | screenshot |
| CF-141 | Every figure must have a ?press= run of its main sequence shot at two or three time budgets, at the default view, from behind (yaw 225) and in see-through. | SKILL.md L181-182; colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L952, §4.6 L997-998 | must | screenshot |
| CF-142 | Every press run must include one out-of-order press, and the figure must answer it with a sensible notice. | SKILL.md L182-183; colorfigures-guide.md §4.6 L997-998, §4.3 L963-965 | must | e2e |
| CF-143 | Every figure with a mechanism must have a 4× close-up in see-through (device scale 4) showing every part held by something, every drive touching what it drives and wires running port to port. | SKILL.md L184; colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L954, §4.6 L999 | must | screenshot |
| CF-144 | Every figure must have a full-page phone shot at 390 × 844 with xr=1&labels=1 showing readable badges, the label list, nothing cut off and no horizontal scroll. | SKILL.md L185; colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L953, §4.6 L1000 | must | screenshot |
| CF-145 | Each shot should run in a fresh browser context with Playwright's fake clock installed and paused before navigation, then run for the shot's budget plus 350 ms of real time for CSS transitions, so results are deterministic. | colorfigures-guide.md §4.1 L914, §4.3 L945-946 | should | unit |
| CF-146 | The suite must write report.json per shot with data-errors, the NaN, Infinity and undefined counts inside #art only, console and page errors, label pills that overlap or leave the viewBox, horizontal page scroll, the active notice and a window.__st snapshot, and exit with code 1 on any error. | colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L956-959 | must | unit |
| CF-147 | A figure must count as passing only when report.json shows no errors, no NaN or undefined in #art and no overlapping pills at any shot. | SKILL.md L186; colorfigures-guide.md §4.6 L1001 | must | unit |
| CF-148 | The suite should put every shot on one contact.png sheet so a reviewer opens one image and zooms only into shots that look wrong. | colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L958-959 | should | screenshot |
| CF-149 | Shots must be read at 2-3× crops of each part, not only as whole images. | SKILL.md L178-179; colorfigures-guide.md §4.6 L995 | must | review |
| CF-150 | ?press= must click buttons by Label@ms and pick chips by Label>Choice@ms (comma-separated, ms after mount), and an unknown label or choice must be written to data-errors so the run fails. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.11 L298-300, §4.3 L963-965; SKILL.md L181 | must | e2e |
| CF-151 | The figure's live state must be readable as window.__st in tests. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.11 L306; SKILL.md L187 | must | unit |
| CF-152 | Out-of-order presses in a test run should be spaced from the figure's own notices, since the press clock starts at mount and a later notice replaces an earlier one. | colorfigures-guide.md §4.3 L967-969 | should | review |
| CF-153 | The bridge must be verified end to end by a probe that sends every host command and checks every figure event, and by an iframe probe at 760 × 560, 760 × 900, 1000 × 520 and 350 × 640 that checks the layout, keyboard focus and pointer capture past the iframe edge. | colorfigures-guide.md §4.5 L989-991, §2.2 L405-427 | must | e2e |
| CF-154 | Verification scripts should use Python Playwright (p.chromium.launch()) or the cached chrome-headless-shell, run Python with -I and keep scripts outside the folders they read; Brave headless hangs and Google Chrome is not installed here. | colorfigures-guide.md §4.1 L910-921 | should | review |
| CF-155 | A figure must switch to turn:'full' only after every angle, press, close-up, phone and report check above has passed. | colorfigures-guide.md §4.6 L1002; SKILL.md L187 | must | review |

## CF · The top 25 pitfalls (must not)

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CF-156 | must not draw separate parts in a fixed order or concatenate several parts in one helper string (pitfall 1); every separate part must go through byDepth as its own item, and helpers must return items. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1010; SKILL.md L70-74 | must | screenshot |
| CF-157 | must not leave depth-sorted items without a box, which sorts them by centre point (pitfall 2); every item must get a tight box, and long or wrapping parts must be split into pieces no longer than the things beside them (wires every 3-7 units). | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1011, §3.3 L689-690; SKILL.md L82-83 | must | screenshot |
| CF-158 | must not rely on byDepth being transitive for boxes that overlap on every axis (pitfall 3); boxes must be made disjoint along some axis, sorted per layer when the camera always looks down, a big block inserted after the last item wholly behind it, and rotated assemblies sorted in local coordinates with SL.byDepthIn(Rᵀ · CAM_V, items). | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1012, §1.4 L132-134, §3.3 L689-706 | must | unit |
| CF-159 | must not nest byDepth where items interleave, because the inner result becomes one string with a fixed position (pitfall 4); sort once at the level where items interleave and nest only groups wholly on one side of everything else. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1013, §1.4 L137 | must | review |
| CF-160 | must not draw a face that is turned away, or draw what is mounted on a face (buttons, decals, displays, knobs, their labels) regardless of it (pitfall 5); test vis(n) for every face and its contents, drawing mounted things after the body while the face is toward the viewer and before it when it turns away. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1014; SKILL.md L75-77 | must | screenshot |
| CF-161 | must not mix tone and toneI on one body (box defaults to toneI), which makes its faces disagree (pitfall 6); pass tone for outside surfaces and toneI for insides and props, consistently. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1015, §1.7 L190 | must | screenshot |
| CF-162 | must not use concave profiles in loft or extrude, whose strips flip and vanish (pitfall 7); build concave parts from convex pieces, each with its own ink and no ink on the joins. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1016; SKILL.md L87-88 | must | screenshot |
| CF-163 | must not leave missing ends and caps (extrude draws sides only, cylX caps only the visible end, a flat disc has no thickness) (pitfall 8); draw the cap on the visible end, give plates and wheels thickness and a rim, and give open tubes a rim and a dark bore. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1017; SKILL.md L84-86 | must | screenshot |
| CF-164 | must not put text on the wrong face (tx works only on +x faces, txY only on +y faces), draw text before its face, or let top text turn upside down from behind (pitfall 9); build other faces' matrices with Dv, draw text after its face and flip top text that must stay readable. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1018, §1.7 L195-197, L205-207; SKILL.md L94 | must | screenshot |
| CF-165 | must not insert unescaped text (&, <, > or ") into SVG text, data-t attributes or button labels written with innerHTML, translations included (pitfall 10); escape at the point of insertion, and the bridge must escape button labels. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1019, §1.7 L207, §1.10 L254, §2.3 L548-549, §3.1 L629 | must | unit |
| CF-166 | must not size label pills by text.length · fs · .62 for every script (Chinese "双金属片" is 19.1 wide in an 18.0 pill; Hindi pills come out twice too wide) (pitfall 11); the three width formulas in the vendored core (core.js:208, :513, :526) must use a measured width (measureText in the same font, cached per string), and labels must stay short. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1020, §2.3 L543-547 | must | unit |
| CF-167 | must not declare a top-level name that core already owns, redeclare a core function, or shadow a core helper with a local name in a function that calls it (pitfall 12); one SL namespace, IIFE-wrapped files and prefixed helpers, with the shot suite catching a blank page through data-errors. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1021, §1.14 L337-366, §3.1 L622 | must | unit |
| CF-168 | must not pass a colour string where an {l, m, d} material is expected (rod(…, INK, …) throws inside mix) or a colour that is not six-digit hex (#abc, red or rgb() give #NaNNaNNaN) (pitfall 13); use six-digit hex only and make materials with matOf('#hex'). | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1022, §1.1 L68-71 | must | unit |
| CF-169 | must not draw ink with style="stroke-width:…", which inkScale does not rescale (pitfall 14); ink must use stroke-width attributes, and the style form is kept for strokes meant in model units (glows, cords). | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1023, §1.8 L209-214 | must | screenshot |
| CF-170 | must not let clip and def ids collide (clipTo ids k1…kN restart every render) or put two figures in one document (pitfall 15); own clipPath, gradient and filter ids must be unique and prefixed, one figure per document. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1024, §1.7 L203 | must | unit |
| CF-171 | must not use #41538f for anything but cast shadows, since build.py moves every fill of that colour out of translucent and clip groups (a clipped shadow in a ghosted group spills off its board, a blue part jumps to full opacity) (pitfall 16); use SHADOW only for cast shadows, drawn first at top level. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1025, §2.1 L400 | must | screenshot |
| CF-172 | must not cast one shadow hull over separate parts or a concave object, which turns them into one blob (pitfall 17); call castShadow once per part or support, clip it to the board top, and cast see-through cages at opacity .45. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1026; SKILL.md L109-111 | must | screenshot |
| CF-173 | must not attach event listeners to drawn SVG elements (they die with the next frame) or leave an act name without a handler (it swallows the drag) (pitfall 18); drawn parts respond only through act(name, …) with a cfg.acts handler, on pointerdown. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1027, §1.10 L280-282; SKILL.md L145-146 | must | e2e |
| CF-174 | must not give a drawn part a hit area big enough to block orbiting (pitfall 18); keep act areas small (transparent push zones) and give every act an equivalent button for keyboard users. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1027; SPEC.md L129 | must | e2e |
| CF-175 | must not run two loops on one state, pass dt in the wrong unit or step physics at frame rate (pitfall 19); one state object and a running flag per loop, dt in seconds clamped to 0.05 s, and physics sub-stepped at 10 ms. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1028, §1.10 L272 | must | unit |
| CF-176 | must not depend on ?press= for labels that share a prefix, contain ",", "@" or ">", are keypad keys, or are translated (pitfall 20); give buttons stable ids pressed through the bridge, run tests in English and use figure params for keypad-only states. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1029, §1.11 L298-303 | must | review |
| CF-177 | must not leave anything outside size, because the frame is fitted once at mount at the default view (lifted lids, exploded stacks and cords spill out, a resize never re-frames, FIG_A is read once) (pitfall 21); put the extremes in size, give the iframe a fixed aspect and set FIG_A before mount. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1030, §1.10 L259-264; SKILL.md L64-65 | must | screenshot |
| CF-178 | must not take or trust a "from behind" shot without turn:'full' or ?free=1, since ?yaw=200 is silently clamped to 105° (pitfall 22); the shot suite must always add free=1, and figures ship with turn:'full' once every angle passes. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1031, §1.3 L105-106 | must | e2e |
| CF-179 | must not rebuild an expensive scene every frame without limits (measured: 0.7 ms per render at 106 paths, 2.7 ms at 491, 3.2 ms at 526, with the hand filter costing far more on phones) (pitfall 23); stop loops when idle, cache item strings that do not change for the current camera, use fewer ring segments on small parts and few clipPaths, and avoid O(n³) sorts and per-frame layout reads. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1032, §3.1 L624-625 | must | unit |
| CF-180 | must not use a figure as a world view (huge drawings make WebKit skip the filter, the filter is tuned to one INK_K, and the camera fights the scene) (pitfall 24); one object per figure, with the flight range as a separate component. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1033, §3.5 L900-904 | must | review |
| CF-181 | must not remount mountFigure in one document, which stacks window listeners and frame drivers (after N remounts one arrow key starts N camera moves) (pitfall 25); one mount per document, with a board or craft switch changing the iframe src. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1034, §3.1 L623 | must | e2e |
| CF-182 | must not grep the whole DOM for NaN (bakeNoise's base64 PNG gives false hits) or use the '*' target origin outside file:// tests (pitfall 25); check #art only and keep '*' for file tests. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1034, §1.9 L226-228, §4.4 L984-985 | must | unit |
| CF-183 | must not pass a box where xrCallouts expects bounds [x0, x1, y0, y1, z0, z1], a colour string where openShell and loft expect a colour function, or path strings to shape (which takes 3D points, while fill, face and ink take path strings). | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1036-1037, §1.4 L128, §1.7 L185-187, §1.12 L316 | must | unit |
| CF-184 | must not give a depth-sorted item an at point that disagrees with its box, because at is used for the tie-break even when a box is present. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.4 L135 | must | unit |
| CF-185 | must not read ui in the first render when the figure wrote const ui = mountFigure(…) (temporal dead zone) or call xrMount before ui exists; guard the first render and call xrMount after mount. | colorfigures-guide.md §5 L1038-1039 | must | unit |

## CF · Engine pattern, reserved names, architecture and Appendix E defects

| id | statement | source | priority | verify |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| CF-186 | must not copy: the originals' fixed-90° core (VIEW 0-3 in 90° steps, centre-only byDepth, hard shading thresholds, txM without a mirror test, pill-only tag); every figure must use the skill's free-camera core with boxed items, smooth tone bands, mirror-tested text and phone badges. | colorfigures-guide.md §0 L43-48, §3.1 L621, §3.2 L633-644 | must | review |
| CF-187 | must not copy: the originals' global engine names (B, st, ui, G, bx, by, PART, DR, D, BATTS, MOTORS, PROPS, FIXED, PARTS, MPOS) shared across script files; each engine must publish through the one SL namespace and every engine and figure file must be an IIFE. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.1 L588, L622; §1.14 L365-366 | must | review |
| CF-188 | must not port the drone core's cfg.frame {fill, left} framing option, which the skill core lacks; frame through size, center and FIG_A. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.2 L643, §1.10 L259-264 | must | review |
| CF-189 | Figure code must not declare at top level any name core.js declares (the complete list in the guide §1.14, CAM_K and _clipN included): a const or let clash is a SyntaxError that leaves a blank page with the message in data-errors. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.14 L339-349, L359-361 | must | unit |
| CF-190 | Figure code must not declare a function named like a core function (setCam, setLight, loft, hull, box, extrude, cyl, cylX, rod, board, tag, bakeNoise, mountFigure, xrCallouts, xrGhost, xrMount, xrGhostRings, xrWrap, mountKeypad), which silently replaces it for core too (the test cylinder lost its sides: 10 paths instead of 22). | colorfigures-guide.md §1.14 L362 | must | unit |
| CF-191 | Figure code must not declare a local name that shadows a core helper called earlier in the same function (a local const on makes core on() throw ReferenceError). | colorfigures-guide.md §1.14 L363; Appendix E L1522 | must | unit |
| CF-192 | Figure code must not reuse the page's other reserved names: window.__st, window.__ln, FIG_A, LBL_U and GAME_KEYS except as documented; element ids fig, art, fdefs, hand, wob, btns, stats, toast, readout, wb, pals, vreset, vspin, lblctl, lbl, xrb, lblb; clip ids k1…kN; core classes (stage, actions, stepwrap, act, car, chips, chip, current, off, stat, k, v, bump, tag, on, vset, vrow, mcap, kpad, dp, hub, go, down, sw, ctl, row, controls, made-with, toast, show), build.py's zwrap, zchip, zm, zp, zi, lgd, and html.nolabels / html.zoomed. | colorfigures-guide.md §1.14 L352-355 | must | review |
| CF-193 | Part drawings must be shared (one drawing per part kind or piece: SMD, THT, connectors and modules for boards; motors, props, stack boards, battery, XT60 with capacitor, camera, VTX, RX and GPS for drones) so every board and craft gets the same look and a fix lands once. | SPEC.md L125; colorfigures-guide.md §3.1 L606, §3.3 L659, L664, §3.5 L858 | must | review |
| CF-194 | Part ids, pin names, nets and each figure's list of modes must live once in data/*.json, imported by the host for lessons, schematic, sketch runner and translations and inlined into the figure page as SL.data. | SPEC.md L154; colorfigures-guide.md §3.3 L665-666, L682-684 | must | unit |
| CF-195 | State should change only through the engine's actions (SL.bench.actions, SL.drone.actions), called alike by buttons, acts, keys and bridge commands, all speaking through one say(msg, ms, sfx). | colorfigures-guide.md §3.3 L686-687, §3.4 L719-720, §3.5 L844 | should | unit |
| CF-196 | A figure should be mostly data (parts with footprints, kinds, build groups, polarity and text) plus a few small hooks, with one template figure per engine to copy. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.1 L603-605, L615 | should | review |
| CF-197 | Faults should be declared as data (polarity options, fault kind, message, fix) rather than per-board or per-craft code. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.1 L609, §3.4 L716, L780-781 | should | review |
| CF-198 | Board programs should be functions of time, out(st, t), plus a once-a-second serial generator, so runs are deterministic and screenshot-friendly. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.1 L610-611, §3.4 L738 | should | unit |
| CF-199 | Each engine should keep one state object and one loop with a generation counter that kills stale loops. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.1 L590, L612 | should | unit |
| CF-200 | The originals' URL test hooks must keep working (board: bare=1 nothing placed and no auto-plug, ex=1 exploded, usb=0 assembled but unplugged, flip, sel, mode; drone: fly, fpv, pose, auto). | colorfigures-guide.md §3.1 L613-614; robotics-bench-a.md L600-602 | must | e2e |
| CF-201 | The embedded-mode bare=1 flag (hide the figure's controls) must not collide with the original board hook bare=1 (start with nothing placed); one of them must be renamed and the choice recorded in docs/DECISIONS.md. | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L441, §3.1 L613; robotics-bench-a.md L600 | must | e2e |
| CF-202 | The board breadboard sheet must be a real isometric Colorfigures scene (a breadboard on the mat, the board or module in its strips, components on named holes such as e12, jumpers from pins to holes) whose connectivity is derived from the strips (5-hole columns, the rails), so the engine checks what is actually wired (LED polarity, a missing resistor, a short between rails) and raises faults from it. | SPEC.md L89, L154; colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L788-794 | must | e2e |
| CF-203 | The board schematic must be generated from the board's nets and pins, rendered in the host in the hand-inked look, or, if drawn in the figure, in viewBox units without P() with orbiting stopped by a capture-phase pointerdown listener on .stage. | SPEC.md L89, L154; colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L795-798 | must | e2e |
| CF-204 | Hovering a net in the schematic should highlight it on the board figure (set {highlightNet}). | colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L795-796 | should | e2e |
| CF-205 | Every board fault must have a visible effect in the figure (pop: vented top and smoke; hot: pulsing red tint and smoke; dark: the LED stays dark; reverse-barrel: polyfuse or protection open, no power; brownout: the ON LED dips as the board resets; cold joint: a flickering or noisy signal; short: current limit and the smoke stopper lamp), with its message and fix passed to the lesson. | SPEC.md L92; colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L768-781 | must | e2e |
| CF-206 | The Pico figure family must state each variant's facts correctly: Pico RP2040 with 2 MB flash and the LED on GP25; Pico W RP2040 with the LED on the wireless chip's WL_GPIO0, not GP25; Pico 2 RP2350 with 4 MB flash and the LED on GP25; all with micro-USB, BOOTSEL and 40 pins at 2.54 mm on a 51 × 21 mm board. | SPEC.md L78, L124; colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L814-816; raspberrypi.com Pico-series documentation (checked 2026-10-24) | must | review |
| CF-207 | The drone engine's craft definition should keep the original part ids FRAME, MOTORS, ESC, WIRES, FC, XT60, CAM, RX, VTX, TOP, BATT and PROPS, which its tests and faults key off. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.5 L856 | should | unit |
| CF-208 | must not copy: a fixed reversed motor in the drone engine's test config (the guide's sketch motors: {needs: ['WIRES', 'FC'], wrong: 2} would keep defect 27, M3 always reversed); which motor is reversed must vary per wiring job. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.5 L857; drone-lab-a.md §10 L1264 | must | unit |
| CF-209 | The drone wiring diagram should be drawn by the host from the craft's wiring data, with selection kept in sync with the figure through part events and set {sel}. | colorfigures-guide.md §3.5 L859, L894-898 | should | e2e |
| CF-210 | must not copy: claw.html's see-through, which passes the mechanism through an xrWrap option that does not exist (over) with mech: () => '', so only the ghost and labels pointing at nothing show; every see-through must draw its mechanism, checked by the mechanism's colours appearing in #art. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix E L1514, §1.12 L320 | must | unit |
| CF-211 | must not copy: SKILL.md's reserved-name list, which misses CAM_K and _clipN and lists camTo, which is not a global; SimLab's name rules must use the complete list taken from core.js. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix E L1515, §1.14 L351 | must | review |
| CF-212 | must not copy: build.py's docstring, which promises a lighter filter while the figure moves on touch screens while the code removes the filter on narrow or touch screens at all times; the vendored builder must document what it actually does. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix E L1516, §2.1 L394 | must | review |
| CF-213 | must not copy: text placed in readout, hint, idea or buttonsLabel (toaster, lamp and claw use them), which the skill core never shows; such text belongs on buttons, labels and stats or in the host lesson. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix E L1517, §1.10 L248-251, §3.2 L640 | must | review |
| CF-214 | must not copy: the baseline FigureFrame setting ready to false in onLoad (FigureFrame.tsx:75-77), which drops every later patch when the figure's ready arrives first; readiness must be set only by ready events. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix E L1518, §2.3 L515-519 | must | e2e |
| CF-215 | must not copy: the baseline FigureFrame's two effects that both post on every patch change, and its '*' target origin (FigureFrame.tsx:35-40, :65-67); each change must be posted once, to window.location.origin. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix E L1519; Appendix D L1483 | must | unit |
| CF-216 | must not copy: Object.assign(st, msg.patch) (board.html:788, drone.html:255), which lets any key from the parent overwrite state (pal: 'x' would crash render); every outside write must go through accept. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix E L1520, §2.3 L524-525 | must | unit |
| CF-217 | must not copy: the baseline schematic sheet passing INK as a material (board.html:564, :523), which makes ?sheet=schematic throw TypeError (reading 'substr'); every sheet must open with empty data-errors. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix E L1521 | must | e2e |
| CF-218 | must not copy: the baseline breadboard sheet's local const on shadowing core on() (board.html:576 with :572), which makes ?sheet=breadboard throw ReferenceError; every sheet must open with empty data-errors. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix E L1522 | must | e2e |
| CF-219 | must not copy: ready sent from a 60 / 40 ms timer (board.html:806, drone.html:267), which causes the ready-before-load race; ready must be posted from mount. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix E L1523 | must | unit |
| CF-220 | The host's figure hook must keep one ready flag, a command queue and the latest event handler in refs, reset ready and the queue when src changes, and pass tsc and an end-to-end handshake test, since the guide's React sketch was never compiled. | colorfigures-guide.md Appendix D L1462-1505 | must | unit |

## Gaps, conflicts and open questions

None of the items below has a source that settles it, so they are not rows yet. Each one needs a decision in
`docs/DECISIONS.md` before the phase that builds it. Once decided, it becomes a row in this list (or in part A).

### Brief needs that no source settles

| gap | brief | what the sources say | decision needed |
|---|---|---|---|
| G01 · Smoke stopper on every drone | SPEC.md L116 | The whoop has no smoke stopper step (drone-lab-a.md §4.4 L366; §9.2 L861, L884). Neither has the Crazyflie (drone-lab-b.md Fig 11 L2286). The DLA and DLB rows follow the originals. | Add a smoke-stopper step to both crafts, or record why they are exempt. |
| G02 · Five live calculator values on every drone | SPEC.md L114 | The wing shows weight, thrust to weight, cruise, stall and top speed and one cruise time, with no max current and no full-throttle time (drone-lab-b.md Fig 09 L1606-1609). The VTOL shows hover and cruise time but no full-throttle time (Fig 10 L1963-1966). | Add max current and a full-throttle time to the wing, add a full-throttle time to the VTOL, and say what hover throttle means on a wing, or record why not. |
| G03 · The brief's build order on crafts that lack a part | SPEC.md L113 | The agrihex has no WIRES part (drone-lab-b.md Fig 08 L1329). The Crazyflie has no XT60, ESC or WIRES part (Fig 11 L2408). The whoop's FC, ESCs and receiver are one AIO board (drone-lab-a.md §9.2 L869, L877). | Decide how frame → motors → ESC → wiring → FC → VTX → camera → receiver → battery → props maps onto these crafts: skip the step, merge it, or show it as built in. |
| G04 · What the drone see-through shows | SPEC.md L23 | The original drone core had no see-through kit (colorfigures-guide.md §0 L43-48; drone-lab-a.md §0 L17). No source lists the inside parts of any craft. | A parts list per craft for its see-through (stack, ESC, capacitor, wiring, battery cells, motor windings, and each craft's own mechanism), written truth-first. |
| G05 · Animation in the wiring view | SPEC.md L16, L23, L115 | The original wiring diagram is static and only selects parts (drone-lab-a.md §5 L461-477). The guide renders it in the host (colorfigures-guide.md §3.5 L894-898). No source says whether lines light up or show current while parts work. | Decide whether the wiring view animates (power on, signal, motor phases) and shows current, as the brief's "light up as they work" suggests. |
| G06 · Which Betaflight version the snippets target | SPEC.md L118 | The snippets mix names from different versions. alt_hold_throttle_deadband (drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L362, Fig 12 L2744) does not exist; Betaflight 4.6 (2025.12) uses alt_hold_deadband. The rows keep the names the research verified. | Pin one Betaflight version (4.5 or 2025.12) and check every Betaflight name against it. |
| G07 · Aux mode ids | SPEC.md L118 | Snippets use mode ids such as aux 2 35 for turtle mode (drone-lab-a.md §9.2 L915) and aux 0 0 / aux 1 1 for ARM / ANGLE (drone-lab-b.md Fig 09 L1727-1733). No source lists the ids for the pinned version. | Check every aux id against the pinned version's mode list. |
| G08 · Hover throttle above 100 % | SPEC.md L114 | When T:W is below 1, the golden data shows hover throttle above 1 (drone-lab-b-calc-matrix.md L106, L111, L295, L301, L307). Only the VTOL clamps it, with min(1, …) (drone-lab-b.md Fig 10 L1958). | Keep the golden display, or show "cannot hover" and record the correction. |
| G09 · Dark mode, languages and accessibility outside the figure plate | SPEC.md L66, L127, L129 | The guide covers the figure plate (colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L530-560). No source covers the flight canvas, the OSD or the host-drawn wiring view. | How the flight view, the OSD and the wiring view look in dark mode, which strings are translated, and how they work from the keyboard and with a screen reader. |
| G10 · How drone progress adds up | SPEC.md L66, L94 | The original keeps no progress (drone-lab-a.md §8 L725). §11 lists what to persist (L1284-1286), and the DLA rows require it, but no source says what the dashboard counts for a craft. | Define each craft's "n of N" for the dashboard. |
| G11 · V and T keys on the controls cards | SPEC.md L117 | The controls cards on Figs 06, 09, 10, 11 and 13 do not list V for FPV (drone-lab-b.md Fig 06 L779, Fig 09 L1840, Fig 10 L2226, Fig 11 L2535, Fig 13 L3196). The VTOL's T key is not listed either (Fig 10 L2114, L2226). The DLB rows keep the cards as written. | Add V (and T on the VTOL) to every card, or record why not. |
| G12 · Angle and acro on every drone | SPEC.md L117 | The GPS, delivery, agrihex, VTOL, Crazyflie and octo crafts fly only autopilot or hold modes (drone-lab-b.md Fig 06 L648-695, Fig 07 L1006-1049, Fig 08 L1357-1394, Fig 10 L2076-2119, Fig 11 L2434-2488, Fig 13 L3072-3117). | Add angle and acro (STABILIZE and ACRO on ArduPilot) where the real firmware has them, or record the exception per craft. |
| G13 · Turtle mode | SPEC.md L117 | The whoop's code sets a turtle switch and its practice asks for turtle flips (drone-lab-a.md §9.2 L915, L946). No source simulates turtle mode. | Simulate turtle mode after a crash, or drop it from the practice task. |
| G14 · Adjustable rates for the racer practice | SPEC.md L117 | The practice asks for 10 % less max rate and 10 more points of expo (drone-lab-b.md Fig 05 L459-461). No source lets the pilot change rates or expo in the simulator. | Add rate and expo controls to acro, or change the task. |
| G15 · Battery C ratings | SPEC.md L114, L124 | Defect 31 needs max current checked against the pack (drone-lab-a.md §10 L1268), but no catalogue gives a C rating (drone-lab-a.md §9.1-9.4; drone-lab-b.md Figs 05-13 design options). | Add real C ratings to every pack, taken from real products. |
| G16 · Low-voltage thresholds per chemistry | SPEC.md L124 | Every pack uses the LiPo thresholds of 3.5 V low and 3.2 V empty (drone-lab-a.md §7.4 L590-591). The chemistry row gives Li-ion only as 4.2 V to 2.8 V (§10 L1243-1244). | Low and empty thresholds for LiHV and Li-ion. |

### Conflicts between sources

| conflict | sources | resolution in this list |
|---|---|---|
| X01 · Hand-inked look on phones | SPEC.md L21 against colorfigures-guide.md §2.1 L394, §5 L1032, Appendix E L1516 | A CF row requires the look on phones too, or an exception recorded in `docs/DECISIONS.md` (for example a lighter filter while the figure moves). Still needs that decision. |
| X02 · Schematic and wiring drawn by the host | SPEC.md L16, L122, L154 against colorfigures-guide.md §3.4 L795-798, §3.5 L894-898 | The host may draw them, but only with the same ink filter, wobble, paper grain and palettes, from the same data (CF and DLA rows). |
| X03 · Wobble forced to 0 in the original Drone Lab | SPEC.md L21 against drone-lab-a.md §1.1 L56 | Must not copy: drone figures open with the default wobble (DLA row). |
| X04 · Smoke stopper on the whoop and Crazyflie | SPEC.md L116 against drone-lab-a.md §9.2 L884, drone-lab-b.md Fig 11 L2286 | Open: see G01. |
| X05 · The skill's three-round cap | SKILL.md L34 against SPEC.md L141 | The brief wins: iterate until correct (CF row). |
| X06 · Two meanings of bare=1 | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L441 against robotics-bench-a.md L600 | One of them must be renamed (CF row). Which one is still open. |
| X07 · The guide's fixed reversed motor | colorfigures-guide.md §3.5 L857 against drone-lab-a.md §10 L1264 | Must not copy: the reversed motor varies per wiring job (CF and DLA rows). |
| X08 · Embedded mode hides the figure controls | colorfigures-guide.md §2.3 L447, L477-489 against drone-lab-a.md §1.1 L56-58 | The wobble slider, swatches, Front view, Turntable and Labels must stay available (CF row). The bridge has no wobble or turntable command yet, so it needs one, or those controls stay in the figure. |
| X09 · Header hint and "Workshop" caption | drone-lab-a.md §1.1 L53-54, §1.4 L121-122 against SKILL.md L156, colorfigures-guide.md §1.10 L251, Appendix E L1517 | The host draws the "Fig. NN — name" line with its hint, and the caption if it is kept. The figure page carries neither (DLA and CF rows). |

### Open questions carried over from drone-lab-b §4

| question | source | how this list answers it |
|---|---|---|
| Keep the original's snippet mistakes, or correct them? | drone-lab-b.md §4 L3348-3349 | Correct them and keep the comment style (DLB cross-cutting rows). |
| Simulate what the lessons teach but the original never simulates? | drone-lab-b.md §4 L3350-3352 | Where drone-lab-b lists the gap as a defect, the fix is must: ArduPilot failsafes, a pump rate tied to ground speed, tail-servo yaw and a GPS fix that can be lost. The rest are should: hex motor-out, spray drift, terrain following, RTK against plain GPS, compass faults, and Flow-deck and Lighthouse limits (DLB rows). |
| VTOL battery: calculator or physics? | drone-lab-b.md §4 L3353 | They must share one model (DLB row). Which number to keep is open. |
| Fig 07: all three drop zones, or DZ 1 only? | drone-lab-b.md §4 L3354 | All three (DLB row). |
| Wing RTH: tighter orbit, or a new configured radius? | drone-lab-b.md §4 L3355 | They must agree (DLB row). The direction is open. A 22 m circle at 18 m/s needs about 56° of bank, above INAV's 35° default. |
| The live site's bot checkpoint | drone-lab-b.md §4 L3356-3357 | Compare against the downloaded copy, not the live site. |

### Evidence at risk

The golden data and live-run artefacts that back the DLB rows (`dlb-live/` with combos, flights, bench, faults,
world, definitions and the downloaded `live.html`; `dlb-scripts/`; `shots/drone-lab-b/`) sit in the session's
temporary scratch directory (drone-lab-b.md §5 L3359-3370). So do the Drone Lab A shots and the Colorfigures test
pages. They should be copied somewhere durable before that directory is cleared, since the live site now blocks
automated browsers.
